# The Forge
Aliases: Dwarven Forge, Forge, Forgers, Forging
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[The Forge]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 0 149 -68
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Forger]] [[Fred]]
The Forge is an area of [[Dwarven Mines]]. Players can use it to refine certain items into their refined form, and create powerful gear, items, [[Accessories]] and even [[Pets]]. [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain Tier 2]] is required to use it. Players have a limited amount of processes available to them at a single time, with additional slots and new recipes unlocking with [[Heart of the Mountain]] progression.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The Forge, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The Forge
■ Refine ores and cast items at the Forge!
Despite the area having no requirements, [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]] is required to Fast Travel there, except on [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]].
## Mechanics
- Forging processes cannot be canceled once started.
- Players begin with 2 Forge slots at [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain Tier 2]]. From there, each [[Heart of the Mountain]] tier grants another forge slot, up to Tier 7.
- Forge Time may be decreased by the following sources, stacking additively.
|Forge Time Reduction|Type of Source|Source|Condition|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|-10.5% → -30%|HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk|Quick Forge ([[Heart of the Mountain]])||Updates retroactively.|
|-25%|Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) perk|[[Cole]]'s Molten Forge|Forging Processes started after the beginning of his term.||
|-0.5 second / Perfect Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) / Kill|[[Gauntlet]]|[[Gemstone Gauntlet]]'s Kinetic ability|[[Mining Islands]]|Applied after all of the above. Cooldown of 1 second.|

## Trivia
- Upgrading an item in the forge, such as Drill or Lantern, keeps all modifications on the item, such as [[Reforging|Reforge]] or [[Enchantments]].
- In the [[Crystal Hollows]], there is a structure that serves as a miniature version of The Forge.
- Forge can be accessed remotely by contacting [[Fred]] via [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].

# The Garden
Aliases: Crop Milestone, Crop Milestones, Crop milestone, Desk (The Garden), Garden, Garden Crop Upgrades, Garden Level, Garden Milestone, Garden Milestones, Garden Plot, Garden Plots, Garden milestone, Garden milestones, Garden plot, Garden plots, Greenhouse Upgrades, Plot, Plots, SkyMart, Skymart, The Desk, Visitor Milestones, Visitor milestones
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Garden
[The Garden]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 5 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Garden ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
← Previous Location: [[Private Island]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Anita]] [[Carpenter]] [[Feast Chef Ted]] [[Jacob]] [[Jeff]] [[Sam]] [[Pesthunter Phillip]] [[Pesthunter Pamela]]
The Garden is an island where players can [[Farming|farm]]. It is similar to the [[Private Island]] in that each player has their own and they can build on it. The player must be [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 5]] to access The Garden.
## Mechanics
### Levels
The Garden has 15 different levels. Leveling it gives [[SkyBlock Levels|10 XP]] per, unlocks new visitors, barn skins, new crops, crop upgrades and faster [[Crop Growth]]. It is leveled up with Garden XP, obtained from Garden Milestones and accepting offers from Visitors.

The menu can be accessed with /gardenlevels, /gardenlevelsmenu (both of which can only be accessed while on the Garden), or by going to the [[Farming|Farming Skill]] menu, where the icon is located at the bottom of the menu, above the "Go Back" and "Close" buttons. It can only be accessed after the player has visited the Garden at least once.
Unlike [[Skills]], there is no "Level 0", meaning that the player starts at [[The Garden|Garden I (1)]]. Additionally, Level 1 is automatically unlocked when visiting the Garden for the first time after it is unlocked (reaching SkyBlock Level 5). Also, there are only 15 levels ( [[The Garden|Garden I (1)]] – [[The Garden|Garden XV (15)]]).
### Crops
Different crops can be grown and harvested in The Garden, but require a Garden level to be unlocked. Nether Wart and Mushroom growth speed is also increased in the Garden, on top of the increase in growth speed gained by the Garden level.
The average amount for each base drop is MinDrops+MaxDrops2.

### Crop Growth
The [[Crop Growth]] influences how fast crops planted in the Garden grow. Every +1 [[Crop Growth]] increases the growing speed by +1%, up to a maximum of 210 [[Crop Growth]] at Garden 15, [[Attributes|Crop Speed X]] Attribute ( [[Locust Shard]]) and [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone]] Ⅵ. As for plants growing in the [[Greenhouse]], the conversion is reduced to +0.25% growing speed per +1 [[Crop Growth]].
Crops in The Garden do not require light to grow at maximum speed, but they do require water. Crops will not grow at all on completely dry soil (even with [[Bone Meal]] or [[Enchanted Bone Meal]]), and will receive a -40% growth speed debuff on soil that is not fully moist.
[[Pumpkin]] and [[Melon Slice]] plants only require water for the stem to grow. After it is grown, [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] and [[Melon]] will sprout regardless of soil moisture.
#### Crop Growing Mechanics
Every 10 game ticks (0.5s) the server sends a number of random ticks to randomly selected crop blocks within a plot. If a crop receives a random tick, it grows by one growth stage. If a crop is already fully grown, nothing happens. Due to this behavior, harvested crops replenish fastest when no crops are yet fully grown, and slowest when almost all crops are already grown.
Crops only grow if the player is in the Garden, regardless of distance to the plot. They do not grow if the player is outside the Garden or offline.
### Pests
After reaching Garden 5, [[Pests|Garden Pests]] will have a chance of appearing whenever a [[Player]] breaks a crop in The Garden. There are 13 types of pests that can spawn. When 4 or more pests are on The Garden, a [[Player]]'s [[Farming Fortune]] will decrease up to 8 pests. [[Farming Fortune]] can be restored by capturing the pests, or can be avoided by having more [[Bonus Pest Chance]]. Pests can also be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]].
### The Desk
The Desk is a key feature of The Garden, which can be found at the barn plot. It allows the [[Player]] to configure Plots, add Crop Upgrades, access SkyMart, view Crop Milestones, and view and change the Barn's Skin. It can also be opened using the /desk [[Commands|command]] (only works while in The Garden).
The cost of your garden plots that you may have will likely be different than the ones shown above in the UI. This is because the costs shown in the above UI are if the plots are bought in numerical order. The cost of a plot depends on the "tier" of the plot and how many plots you have already unlocked in that "tier", not the plot number. The "tier" of a specific plot is determined by its [[The Garden|Garden]] Level requirement, which can either be 1, 3, 5, or 7. In addition, the cost(s) for all remaining plot(s) that have not been unlocked in a specific tier will all be the same, unlike the UI above, where the plot costs are all different. This means that if n plots have been bought in a single tier, the cost for every other plot in the tier will be cost of the nth plot of the tier, shown above in the UI.
For example, if Plots 1, 2, and 3 were bought, Plot 4 will cost Compost x8. But if Plots 1 and 3 were only bought, the costs for both Plots 2 and 4 will be Compost x4, as that is the cost in the UI for Plot 3, after two "Tier 1" plots have already been bought. The costs for plots in other tiers will remain unaffected, as they are only affected by other plots in their "tier".
### Composter
The Composter is a feature that allows the player to turn crops and other [[Composter|Organic Matter]] into [[Compost]]. It requires [[Machine Fuel]] such as [[Biofuel]] to operate. It can create 1 Compost every 10 minutes, with each Compost requiring 4000 Organic Matter and 2000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].
### Plots
The Garden has 24 Plots in a 5x5 grid, with the Barn in the middle. Each plot is 96x96 blocks. To unlock a plot, the player has to spend [[Compost]] or [[Compost Bundle|Compost Bundles]] at the Desk. There is also a [[The Garden|Garden Level]] requirement. After unlocking the plot, it needs to be cleaned. [[Plot Eraser]] can clean the plot instantly. [[Sam's Scythe]] or a [[Garden Scythe]] can be used to break plants faster. Leaves will decay once the plot is over 70% clean. Breaking the following blocks on the plot counts towards cleaning progress:
- [[Dirt]] and [[Grass|Grass Blocks]] above ground level
- [[Stone]]
- [[Flowers]]
- [[Long Grass]]
- [[Double Tallgrass]]
- [[Wood (disambiguation)|Wood]]
- [[Leaves]]
After cleaning the plot, the player can paste a preset onto it at the Desk, which will overwrite the blocks on the plot. The player can use default presets for crops, which use the specific crop's corresponding "seed" item. The seeds can be obtained through the [[Bazaar]] if the player has a [[Booster Cookie]] active, or from the player's inventory and [[Sacks]]. The player can also save plots as custom presets, which will only paste non-crop blocks and will not drop blocks broken by the player.
[[Melon Slice|Melon Slices]] and [[Pumpkin]] blocks in the player's inventory will be automatically converted to seeds. Enchanted forms of crops can also be used to paste plots, and the difference will be refunded into the player's inventory once pasting is done. (Note: While this is supposed to work with sacks too, it currently only works with crops in the player's inventory.)

### Crop Upgrades
Crop Upgrades increase the player's [[Crop Fortune]] while harvesting certain crops. Each crop can be upgraded at the Desk for [[Copper]], and each upgrade grants +5 [[Crop Fortune]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Requirement|Cost|Cumulative Cost|
|-|-|-|-|
|Tier I|Garden II (2) ([[The Garden]])|5 [[Copper]]|5 [[Copper]]|
|Tier II|Garden IV (4) ([[The Garden]])|10 [[Copper]]|15 [[Copper]]|
|Tier III|Garden VI (6) ([[The Garden]])|20 [[Copper]]|35 [[Copper]]|
|Tier IV|Garden VIII (8) ([[The Garden]])|50 [[Copper]]|85 [[Copper]]|
|Tier V|Garden X (10) ([[The Garden]])|100 [[Copper]]|185 [[Copper]]|
|Tier VI|Garden XII (12) ([[The Garden]])|500 [[Copper]]|685 [[Copper]]|
|Tier VII|Garden XIII (13) ([[The Garden]])|1,000 [[Copper]]|1,685 [[Copper]]|
|Tier VIII|Garden XIV (14) ([[The Garden]])|2,000 [[Copper]]|3,685 [[Copper]]|
|Tier IX|Garden XV (15) ([[The Garden]])|4,000 [[Copper]]|7,685 [[Copper]]|
### Visitors
Visitors are [[NPC|NPCs]] across [[SkyBlock]] that occasionally visit the [[Player]]'s Garden. The player will receive an offer from them. Offers can be accepted or declined.
Visitors will stay in the Garden until the player accepts or declines their offer. A maximum of 5 Visitors can be in the Garden at one time.
All Visitors come with a rarity tag. The higher the rarity of a Visitor, the rarer it is to have them visit.
#### Requests
When visiting, Visitors will request varying amounts of certain items. The quantity of the items that a Visitor requests is determined by a base number of items, the player's [[The Garden|Garden]] level, and the visitor's rarity.
#### Rewards
Visitors offer [[Farming|Farming XP]], [[The Garden|Garden XP]], [[Copper]], [[Bits]], and sometimes other unique rewards to the player if they accept the visitor's request.
All Visitors also have a chance to give one of the following items:
- [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]] (3%)
- [[Garden Chips|Quickdraw Chip]] (3%)
- [[Flowering Bouquet]] (2%)
- [[Overgrown Grass]] (0.1%)
- [[Green Bandana]] (0.4%, RARE Visitors and above only)
#### Visitor's Logbook
The Visitor's Logbook keeps track of who has visited the player's Garden, and requirements for NPCs to visit.
## SkyMart
SkyMart is a shop accessible through the Desk. It allows the player to purchase Farming-related [[Equipment]], [[Tools]], [[Barn Skins]] and more. Most of the items are purchased with [[Copper]], but [[Coins]] and [[SkyBlock Gems]] are also used as currencies.
### Garden Milestones
[[Farming]] Experience obtained from Garden Milestones is not affected by [[Farming Wisdom]].
#### Crop Milestones
Crop Milestones can be reached by harvesting certain amounts of crops. When milestones are reached, they grant [[The Garden|Garden XP]], [[Farming|Farming XP]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. Crop Milestones can only be leveled up while farming on The Garden. Any crops broken outside The Garden will not count towards this milestone (Private Island, Barn, etc.). Additionally crops farmed by any means that are not the player do not count towards Crop Milestones even though they still count towards [[Collections|Collection]] progress.
Crop Milestones have 46 tiers per crop. Your progress towards tier 46 on a specific crop will be shown once you reach tier 20 in that crop.
- [[The Garden/Crop Milestones|Tab 1]]
#### Visitor Milestones
There are 2 types of Visitor Milestones:
- Unique Visitors Served, which increases based on the amount of offers from unique Visitors the player has accepted. This functionally maxes out at tier 17.
- Offers Accepted, which increases based on the amount of offers from Visitors the player has accepted
Both grant [[The Garden|Garden XP]], [[Farming|Farming XP]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] when milestones are reached.

### Barn Skins
Barn Skins are a type of cosmetic that changes the look of the Barn. They can be applied to the Barn at the Desk.
### Greenhouse
After reaching [[The Garden|Garden VII (7)]], the [[Carpenter]] can visit The Garden. Completing their offer for the first time grants the [[Greenhouse Blueprint]], which can be given to [[Sam]] to unlock the [[Greenhouse]].
The [[Greenhouse]] allows the player to plant regular crops and grow [[Mutations]]. Up to 3 [[Greenhouse]] plots can be unlocked and placed.
## Bugs
- During the tutorial quest, the first visitor currently does not appear. Leaving the Garden (and making sure no one else is on it) and then rejoining it about 10 seconds later should make them appear.
## Trivia
- The only [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Visitor is [[Spaceman]]. They are not accessible anywhere in [[SkyBlock]].
- The Fear Mongerer Visitor turns into a bat when leaving the garden.
- Talking to a Visitor while [[Guesting|being a guest]] on someone else's Garden will result in the following dialogue. Hmm... I haven't seen you around here before. Do you work here?.
- Visitors have a variety of lines while waiting in queue, similarly to [[Minions]].
  - One of those lines features one of the visitors asking what is the time, another visitor answers and their dialogue changes with the time. This is the only way to tell SkyBlock time precisely to the minute.
- There is a limit of 64 simultaneous primed [[TNT]] entities on the player's [[Private Island]] and The Garden.
  - This limit was added because of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] SomethingLT.
- Just like on [[Private Island]], dying to the Void doesn't remove [[Coins]] from the player's purse.

# The Lift
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[The Lift]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Lift Operator]] [[Royal Guards]]
The Lift is the first section of the [[Dwarven Mines]] players visit after completion of [[Rhys]]' quest. It can be used by players to talk to the [[Lift Operator]] and access other sections of the [[Deep Caverns]]. It can also be entered from the [[Ironman's Guild]]: however, it serves no purpose there.

|[[Royal Guards]]|[[Royal Guards]]|The Lift; -46, 200, -125 ([[Coordinates]])|Guards the [[Dwarven Mines]].|✕|
|[[Royal Guards]]|[[Royal Guards]]|The Lift; -46, 200, -119 ([[Coordinates]])|Guards the [[Dwarven Mines]].|✕|

# The Park
Aliases: Park, Savanna Gold Mine
Categories: Locations, Park, 0.7.4, Foraging
[The Park]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to The Park]]; [[Travel Scroll to Howling Cave]]; [[Travel Scroll to Jungle Island]]
Level: Foraging I (1) ([[Foraging]])
Sub-locations: [[Birch Park]] [[Spruce Woods]] [[Viking Longhouse]] [[Dark Thicket]] [[Trials of Fire]] [[Howling Cave]] [[Savanna Woodland]] [[Melody's Plateau]] [[Soul Cave]] [[Jungle Island]] [[Spirit Cave]]
Fairy Souls: 12
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
← Previous Location: [[Forest]]
Next Location →: [[Galatea]] [[Moonglade Marsh]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Campfire Adept]] [[Campfire Initiate]] [[Charlie]] [[Gustave]] [[Juliette]] [[Kelly]] [[Master Tactician Funk]] [[Melancholic Viking]] [[Melody]] [[Molbert]] [[Old Shaman Nyko]] [[Romero]] [[Ryan]] [[Vanessa]] [[Worker Xavier]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Pack Spirit]] [[Howling Spirit]] [[Soul of the Alpha]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Acacia Log]] [[Birch Log]] [[Bone]] [[Dark Oak Log]] [[Foraging Exp Boost]] [[Hound Pet]] [[Jungle Log]] [[Oak Log]] [[Spruce Log]] [[Travel Scroll to Howling Cave]] [[Weak Wolf Catalyst]]
Resources: [[Birch Log]] [[Spruce Log]] [[Dark Oak Log]] [[Acacia Log]] [[Jungle Log]]
The Park is the first [[Foraging]] island, consisting of several large floating islands connected by launch pads.
## Locations
The Park is broken up into five areas, each devoted to a type of forest. See the sections below table for a more detailed breakdown.
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Birch Park]]|Requires Foraging I (1) ([[Foraging]]) to access. Serves as a place to harvest Birch Logs ([[Birch Log]]).|[[Charlie]], [[Vanessa]]|
|[[Spruce Woods]]|Requires Foraging II (2) ([[Foraging]]) to access. Serves as a place to harvest Spruce Logs ([[Spruce Log]]).|[[Gustave]], [[Kelly]]|
|^ [[Viking Longhouse]]|Contains the [[Melancholic Viking]]'s shop.|[[Melancholic Viking]]|
|[[Dark Thicket]]|Requires Foraging III (3) ([[Foraging]]) to access. Serves as a place to harvest Dark Oak Logs ([[Dark Oak Log]]).|None|
|^ [[Howling Cave]]|Contains wolf mobs, which are often killed for [[Wolf Slayer]].|[[Old Shaman Nyko]]|
|^ [[Trials of Fire]]|Allows the player to earn and upgrade the [[Campfire Badge]].|[[Campfire Adept]], [[Campfire Initiate]], [[Ryan]]|
|[[Savanna Woodland]]|Requires Foraging IV (4) ([[Foraging]]) to access. Serves as a place to harvest Acacia Logs ([[Acacia Log]]).|[[Master Tactician Funk]], [[Romero]], [[Worker Xavier]]|
|^ [[Soul Cave]]|Contains wolf mobs, which are often killed for [[Wolf Slayer]].|None|
|^ [[Melody's Plateau]]|Features [[Melody]] and the [[Harp]].|[[Melody]]|
|[[Jungle Island]]|Requires Foraging V (5) ([[Foraging]]) to access. Serves as a place to access Jungle Logs ([[Jungle Log]]).|[[Juliette]], [[Molbert]], [[Romero]]|
|^ [[Spirit Cave]]|Contains wolf mobs, which are often killed for [[Wolf Slayer]].|None|
### Birch Park
Birch Park
The [[Birch Park]] is the first section of The Park and requires [[Foraging|Foraging I (1)]].
It contains two [[NPC|NPCs]]: [[Charlie]], who sends new players in search to collect [[Birch Log|Birch Logs]] and get to all the different sections, and [[Vanessa]], who can be paid 5,000 [[Coins]], to make it rain for a minute.
### Spruce Woods
Spruce Woods
The [[Spruce Woods]] is the second section of The Park, and requires completion of the Into the Woods quest to access. It contains three [[NPC|NPCs]]: [[Kelly]], who wants [[Spruce Log|Spruce Logs]], the [[Melancholic Viking]], who the player can buy items from, and [[Gustave]], who gives the Woods Racing quest.
#### Woods Racing
The Woods Race is started by talking to [[Gustave]]. The player must travel to the [[Jungle Island]] and back in continually lesser amounts of time. Each tier completed grants a different reward.
#### Melancholic Viking Quest
The [[Melancholic Viking]] is located on the [[Viking Longhouse]] in the [[Spruce Woods]]. The player can access their shop by using 1 [[Magical Water Bucket]], 1 [[Raw Cod]], and 1 [[Fish Hat]].
### Dark Thicket
Dark Thicket
The [[Dark Thicket]] is the third section of The Park and requires completion of the A Helping Hand quest to access. There are four [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the [[Dark Thicket]]: two [[Campfire Initiate|Campfire Initiates]], one [[Campfire Adept]], and [[Ryan]]. A weak wall leads into the [[Howling Cave]].
#### Trial of Fire
The Trial of Fire can be completed to earn various [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Badges]].
### Savanna Woodland
Savanna Woodland
The [[Savanna Woodland]] is the fourth section of The Park and requires completion of the Trial of Fire quest to enter. There are three [[NPC|NPCs]] located in this section: [[Worker Xavier]], [[Melody]], and [[Master Tactician Funk]]. There is a fourth NPC, [[Romero]], hiding in a cave, who only appears at the beginning of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest.
#### Melody
[[Melody]] is found on [[Melody's Plateau]] in the [[Savanna Woodland]]. They ask for 512 (8 stacks) [[Acacia Log]] to help fix the [[Harp]]. Fixing the [[Harp]] allows the player to complete songs to earn rewards.
#### Master Tactician Funk
[[Master Tactician Funk]] is located in a tent near the edge of the [[Savanna Woodland]]. They sell the [[Tactician's Sword]] and [[Wood Singularity]].
#### Gold Mine
A small gold mine can be mined for [[Ores|Gold Ore]]. During a quest from [[Romero]], mining the [[Ores|Gold Ore]] has a chance to drop a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Yellow Rock]]. There is also a weak wall that leads to the [[Soul Cave]].
### Jungle Island
Jungle Island
The [[Jungle Island]] is the fifth section of The Park and requires completion of The Rebuild [[Quests|Quest]] to enter. There are two NPCs here: [[Juliette]], who longs for [[Romero]], and [[Molbert]], who requests [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]], [[Carrot|Carrots]], and [[String]] to make traps.
Upon completing the [[Romero]] Questline, he moves to the [[Jungle Island]] with [[Juliette]].
All of the main NPCs grant an armor piece:
- [[Charlie]] rewards [[Charlie's Trousers]] for 64 (1 stack) [[Birch Log|Birch Logs]].
- [[Kelly]] rewards [[Kelly's T-Shirt]] for 128 (2 stacks) [[Spruce Log]].
- [[Melody]] rewards [[Melody's Shoes]] for 512 [[Acacia Log]].
- [[Molbert]] rewards the [[Mole Hat]] for 512 [[Jungle Log]].
### Romero and Juliette Quest
The [[Romero and Juliette]] Quest from [[Romero]] and [[Juliette]] requires various [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|items]] to complete. Completing the quest rewards a [[Ring of Love|Legendary Ring of Love]].

## Trivia
- The SkyBlock community has spent over 182B [[Coins]] on rain as of August 28th, 2026.
- Before version 0.23, the higher sections needed higher foraging levels to access: however, they could be reached using parkour, an [[Aspect of the End]], or a [[Grappling Hook]].
  - Now the player is teleported back with the message: You must complete the <quest> to use this!
- There is a video on YouTube featuring the building process of The Park.

# The Wasteland
Categories: Locations
[The Wasteland]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])

The Wasteland is an area on the [[Crimson Isle]] behind the [[Blazing Volcano]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The Wasteland, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The Wasteland
■ Look up.
## Purpose
The location is a barren expanse of [[Netherrack]]. There is an entrance leading off to the [[Smoldering Tomb]], and there is a path leading to [[Scarleton]]. [[Dive Ghast|Dive Ghasts]] spawn in the air above The Wasteland.

# Throne Room
Categories: Locations, Crimson Isle
[Throne Room]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Magetown ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: Community Center ([[Community Center (Scarleton)]])

[Inhabitants]

The Throne Room is a [[Locations|Location]] within the [[Community Center (Scarleton)|Community Center]] in [[Scarleton]]. It is the location of [[Queen Nyx]]. They player cannot enter it unless they are a member of the Mage [[Factions|Faction]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Throne Room, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Throne Room
■ Meet Queen Nyx.

# Time Chamber

[Time Chamber]

Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
← Previous Location: [[Continuum]]

The Time Chamber is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]] where players fight [[Sun Gecko]].
## Purpose
The Time Chamber is an arena where the player can fight [[Sun Gecko]] for [[Sun Gecko Essence]]. [[Sun Gecko Essence]] can be spent at [[Oruo The Almighty]] for special items and perks.

# Time-Torn Isles
Categories: Locations
[Time-Torn Isles]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: -5 145 8
[Metadata]
ID: TIME_TORN_ISLES
The Time-Torn Isles is a [[Locations|Location]] within [[The Mountaintop]] of the [[Rift Dimension]]. It serves as a parkour trial leading to an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Purpose
The Time-Torn Isles can be accessed from the middle of [[The Mountaintop]]. At the starting point, a [[Fleespook]] can be found, which leads the player to several stationary platforms. These platforms are in the air and far apart, but can be accessed by teleporting between the moving platforms with an [[Aspect of the Leech]].
The further-in platforms move faster, but at the final platform, an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] can be found.
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in the Time-Torn Isles:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Fleespook|22.5|208|-4.5|Found at the end of the Time-Torn Isles after following the [[Fleespook]].|
## Tips
- It is recommended to upgrade your [[Aspect of the Leech]] to its maximum power using [[Transmission Tuner|Transmission Tuners]] and [[Super Leech Modifier|Super Leech Modifiers]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Time-Torn Isles.|

# Tomb Floodway
Categories: Locations
[Tomb Floodway]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]])
Music: "Moonglade Marsh ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Coordinates: -577 92 24
← Previous Location: [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Martin]]
[Metadata]
ID: TOMB_FLOODWAY
The Tomb Floodway is a [[Locations|Location]] found on the [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It can be accessed through a passageway located towards the right side of the entrance to the [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]].
## Features
While in the Tomb Floodway, players gain +10 [[Treasure Chance]] and cannot fish up [[Sea Creatures]].
The Tomb Floodway is the only place where players can fish up [[Flexbone|Flexbones]]. These have a 25% chance to replace [[Treasure]] that's caught, with the amount gained depends on the tier of [[Treasure]] fished up:
|Tier|Amount|
|-|-|
|GOOD CATCH|1x|
|GREAT CATCH|2-4x|
|OUTSTANDING CATCH|8x|
The [[Attributes|Flexible Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], obtained from syphoning [[Osedax Shard|Osedax Shards]], increases the chances of catching [[Flexbone|Flexbones]] by +2.5-25%.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Tomb Floodway:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Martin]]|[[Martin]]|Tomb Floodway; -579.5, 93, 31.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about [[Fishing]] in the Tomb Floodway.|✕|

The Torrhus Canyon is a [[Foraging]] [[Locations|Location]] and the second island of [[Galatea]]. It can be accessed from the [[Moonglade Marsh]] after unlocking [[Heart of the Forest|Heart of the Forest IV (4)]].
## Features
### Chapters
The Torrhus Canyon has a total of 63 tasks across 7 chapters. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Helia]].
Begin your journey through Torrhus Canyon.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Miria]]|Miria is one of the Starlyn Sisters. She holds Miria's Contests across Torrhus Canyon each day.|+250 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|Open a Helix Tree ([[Helix Log]]) Gift|Helix Trees are native to Torrhus Canyon. They have higher Toughness than Fig and Mangrove Trees and require you to entirely cut the Beige twin helix before moving on to the Red one to avoid a Sweep Penalty!|64 (1 stack) [[Helix Log]]|
|Loot a Honeyhive ([[Honeyhives]])|Honeyhives can be found around Torrhus Canyon - there's even one right by the entrance of the island! You can reach into them in search of Loot, such as Shards and Honeycomb. They refill after a while and are the main source of the Honeycomb Collection.|16 [[Honeycomb]]|
[Chapter I Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; 1 Honeybuzz Shard; +250 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +2,500 Foraging XP (Foraging); +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]

|Participate in Miria's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|Miria will reward for participating in her daily Starlyn Contests while on Torrhus Canyon!|+500 [[Desert Whispers]]|
|Hunt a [[Bunbun]]|Bunbuns hop freely around Torrhus Canyon. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Hunt a [[Firefox]]|Firefoxes frequently spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. You can hunt them using a Lasso.|+1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|

|Hold a [[Veilshroom Bracelet]]|The [[Veilshroom Bracelet]] is an upgrade to the [[Mangrove Grippers]] that can be crafted upon reaching Ruby Veilshroom Collection IX ([[Ruby Veilshroom]]).|+10,000 Desert Whispers ([[Desert Whispers]])|

[150x (2 stacks plus 22 (150 in total)) Safari Essence 4x First-Class Safari Ticket (Safari Tickets) +4,000 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +20,000 Desert Whispers (Desert Whispers); +60,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +35 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
### Foraging Mechanics
Helix Trees can be chopped to get [[Helix Log|Helix Logs]]. Helix Trees have 150 [[Toughness]] and a cutting style of Beige Logs → Red Logs.
[[Honeycomb]] can be collected from [[Honeyhives]] found around the Island.
[[Ruby Veilshroom]] can be found inside caves in the [[Torrhus Springs]] or dropped by [[Sea Creatures]] fished on the Torrhus Canyon.

## Trivia
- The release of the Torrhus Canyon marks the first time since [[SkyBlock]]'s release where more than one island has been released in one update.
- Despite [[Miria]] implying in her dialogue that you have completed [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]], you can reach the Torrhus Canyon without completing any. Doing so does not change any dialogue.

[Resources]

The Torrhus Heights is a [[Locations|Location]] found on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It represents the higher up portions of the island.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Torrhus Heights, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Torrhus Heights
■ Hunt critters that live up here.
## Features
The Torrhus Heights represents the top portion of the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Players can harvest [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] for [[Helix Log|Helix Logs]] and go [[Hunting]] for unique [[Critters]].
Throughout the area, players can find [[Sneaky Tiki]] totems that, when rotated, will spawn a [[Cheeky Tiki]], [[Shrieky Tiki]], or [[Sneaky Tiki]]. There are also [[Mountain Goat|Mountain Goats]], [[Groundhog|Groundhogs]], and [[Firefox|Firefoxes]], which spawn naturally.
On higher up trees, players can find [[Blue Jay|Blue Jays]], which can be caught using a [[Lassos|Lasso]] after standing still. Similarly, a [[Beeheemoth]] can occasionally spawn in the area.
## NPCs

# Torrhus Springs
Categories: Locations
[Torrhus Springs]

[Inhabitants]

Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Haggard]] [[Brineling]] [[Sprawl]] [[Torrid]] [[Silkbreeze]] [[Giant Isopod]] [[Solar]] [[Water Snake]] [[Ember]] [[Sepialot]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Aquamarine Dye]] [[Bone]] [[Distant Echo]] [[Ember Shard]] [[Enchanted Bone]] [[Enchanted Helix Log]] [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]] [[Helix Log]] [[Helixis]] [[Isopod Husk]] [[Reinforced Netting]] [[Ruby Veilshroom]] [[Sea Brine]] [[Sepialot Shard]] [[Solar Shard]] [[Sublime Silk]] [[Veilshroom Bunch]] [[Water Hyacinth]] [[Water Snake Shard]]
The Torrhus Springs is a [[Locations|Location]] found high up on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It can be accessed from the [[Spring Path]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Torrhus Springs, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Torrhus Springs
■ Go fishing for Torrhus Sea Creatures.
■ Dive down into Torrhus Springs.
■ Hunt mobs that live in and around the Springs!
## Features
The Torrhus Springs contains a tall body of water that players can swim down. The top of it is the [[Spring Shallows]], and lower down, players reach the [[Spring Depths]], which connects to the [[Ant's Cave]], [[Hotspot Haven]], and [[Desert Temple]].
Players can also fish from the water to catch [[Sea Creatures]], such as the [[Haggard]], [[Brineling]], [[Sprawl]], [[Torrid]], [[Silkbreeze]], and [[Giant Isopod]].
## Zones
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Spring Shallows]]|The top portion of the water, containing Solars ([[Solar]]).|None|
|[[Spring Depths]]|The bottom portion of the water, which requires more [[Respiration]] and [[Pressure Resistance]] to survive through. Contains Water Snakes ([[Water Snake]]), Embers ([[Ember]]), and Sepialots ([[Sepialot]]). Also contains pathways to a Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]]), the [[Ant's Cave]], [[Hotspot Haven]], and [[Desert Temple]].|None|
|[[Ant's Cave]]|Contains Ant ([[Ant (NPC)]]) and Ants ([[Ant]]). Accessed from a path in the [[Spring Depths]].|[[Ant (NPC)]]|
|[[Hotspot Haven]]|Contains a Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]]) that lets players fish up the the [[Giant Water Bug Shard]]. Accessed from a path in the [[Spring Depths]].|[[Hunter McNeil]]|
|[[Desert Temple]]|Accessed at the bottom of the [[Spring Depths]] and after completing a color puzzle.|None|

The Tranquility Sanctum is a large cave with [[Bamboo]] and pandas. It can be found behind waterfalls in the [[West Reaches]] and [[Moonglade's Edge]]. During the Spring and Summer seasons in SkyBlock, [[Bambuleaf|Bambuleaves]] spawn. Feeding them [[Bamboo]] grants the [[Attributes|Strong Arms]] attribute shard. # Trials of Fire
Aliases: Campfire, Campfire Trial, Fire Trial, Through the Campfire, Through the campfire, Trial of Fire, Trials Of Fire, Trials of Fire (Zone), Trials of Fire (zone)
Categories: Locations, Outdated pages, Quests, 0.7.4, Park
[Trials of Fire]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Dark Thicket]]
Coordinates: -359 102 -90
Requirements: A Helping Hand Quest
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Campfire Adept]] [[Campfire Initiate]] [[Ryan]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Dark Oak Log]]
The Trials of Fire is a location and challenge found in the [[Dark Thicket]] of [[The Park]]. The player must stand inside a campfire for 10 seconds of increasing damage to earn and improve an [[Accessories|Accessory]].
The Trials of Fire can be started by talking to [[Ryan]]. If the player fails a trial, they do not lose [[Coins]] or potion effects. If the DPS is higher than the player's max [[Health]], the player instantly fails.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Trials of Fire, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Trials of Fire
■ Compete in a Trial of Fire.
## Trials
The [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Badges]] heal varying amounts of [[Health]] per second while burning based on progression. In addition, all [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] versions of the [[Campfire Badge]] grant [[Health]] per second boost regardless of if the player is not on fire.

### General Tips
The campfire deals both [[True Damage]] and [[Damage]]. All defensive stats like [[Defense]], [[Health]] and [[True Defense]] will help in the trial.
Players must rely on [[Health]] and [[Healing]] items. Helpful items include:

### Trial XXX Recommendations
This section needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by Voball (contribs|logs|block) on 1:26, October 27 2026 (13 hours ago)
To survive up to the Trials of Fire XXX, players must have at least 352800 [[Health]] or the equivalent true damage protection. This list assumes that their mana lasts for the duration of the campfire test. There are two commonly used methods to overcome the later trials, which are listed below.
#### Method 1
- Armor: [[Mastiff Armor]] with the [[Precursor Gear|Ancient]] reforge.
  - Optionally, it is possible to put [[Fire Protection]] on all pieces and [[True Protection]] on the chestplate.
- Accessory Power: [[Powers|Commando]], [[Powers|Shaded]], or [[Powers|Silky]] are the best accessory powers when using [[Mastiff Armor]]. It is important to have a good amount of [[Accessories]] to maximize the effect of the accessory powers.
- Pets:
  - A high-level [[Enderman Pet]] of any rarity for [[Crit Damage]].
  - An [[Ender Dragon Pet]], as it's third ability increases both [[Crit Damage]] and [[Health]].
  - [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blue Whale Pet]] can also be used due to its high bonus to [[Health]].
  - Maxed level of [[Sheep Pet]]'s second ability, Overheal, could also add 10% of [[Health]].
  - Another good pet is the [[Mithril Golem Pet]], giving the largest amount of [[True Defense]].
  - Optional Pet Item: [[Iron Claws]]
- Weapons:
  - [[Terminator]] with the [[Reforging|Rapid]] reforge and [[Overload|Overload V]] is the best option for critical damage on a weapon, granting a total of 365 [[Crit Damage]].
  - If the player wishes to use a more affordable weapon, a [[Livid Dagger]] with [[Critical]] enchantment and [[Suspicious Vial|Suspicious]] reforge or a [[Juju Shortbow]] with [[Reforging|Rapid]] reforge are recommended.
- Potion effects especially: Regeneration; Critical; True Defense and Spirit (best option is to use [[God Potion]]).
#### Method 2
- Items: [[Wither Cloak Sword]] with [[Ultimate Wise|Ultimate Wise V]], and [[Mastiff Armor]].
  - [[Stamina Potion|Stamina]] and [[Mana Potion|Mana Potions]] with a [[Brews|Bitter Ice Tea]] base, as well as [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
  - A level 100 [[Jerry Pet]] with a [[Minos Relic]] can be helpful to increase the effects of the Mana potion.
  - Optionally, a [[Gloomlock Grimoire]] can be used in the process.
- Method: Use the Wither Cloak Sword to tank the majority of the damage. If the effect expires, switch to Gloomlock Grimoire and spam between life tap and Extreme Measures. If you still are having trouble try asking someone else to cast the Lesser Orb of Healing and place a Power Orb. Mastiff Armor is still needed to overcome one second of the damage dealt (35280 [[Health]] ).
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Trials of Fire:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Campfire Adept]]|[[Campfire Adept]]|Trials of Fire; -360.5, 102.5, -96.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the Trials of Fire and the campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Campfire Initiate]]|Trials of Fire; -358.5, 102.5, -94.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the Trials of Fire and the campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Campfire Initiate]]|[[Campfire Initiate]]|Trials of Fire; -362.5, 102.5, -97.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the Trials of Fire and the campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Ryan]]|[[Ryan]]|Trials of Fire; -364.5, 102.5, -90.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the Trials of Fire.|✕|

|Dying to the Campfire (Random)|TRIAL OF FIRE! Trial cancelled: You burned like a toast!|
## Trivia
- The Trial of Fire's Campfire checks slightly higher for the player's hitbox than the [[Trial of Blue Flames]]' Soul Campfire.

# Unincorporated
Aliases: High Level, Unincorporated Territory
Categories: Locations
[Unincorporated]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -5 84 153
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Skeleton ([[Skeleton (Unincorporated)]])
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]]
[Metadata]
ID: UNINCORPORATED_TERRITORY
Unincorporated is a [[Locations|Location]] containing a large tall door of wood flanked on both sides by statues. It is located behind the [[Mountain]].
## Purpose
The Unincorporated area, despite its name, is a place where level 6 [[Skeleton (Unincorporated)|Skeleton]] spawn at night. When killed, they drop an extra [[Bone]] that is not affected by Auto Pickup. If this bone is not picked up by a player within a short amount of time, the item will vanish and a skeleton will appear in its place.
Due to the high amount of [[Mob|mobs]] that can spawn, this is a popular spot to grind for [[Purple Candy|Candy]] during the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Mobs
|Mob|Stats|Spawned at/via|Mob Types|Item Drops|Non-Item Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv5] Skeleton ([[Skeleton (Unincorporated)]])|[[Health]]: 200; [[Damage]]: 47|Unincorporated|[[Skeletal]]|3–6x [[Bone]] 0–1x [[Bone Dye]]|+7 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 5 [[Experience]]|

The Verdant Summit is a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Verdant Summit is a small plateau near the [[North Reaches]] with [[Fig Log|Fig Trees]] to chop. Additionally, [[Lushlilac|Lushlilac Bushes]] can be picked for [[Lushlilac|Lushlilacs]], and a river runs through the inside of the hill.

# Viking Longhouse
Categories: Locations
[Viking Longhouse]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Spruce Woods]]
← Previous Location: [[Spruce Woods]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Melancholic Viking]]
The Viking Longhouse is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Viking Longhouse, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Viking Longhouse
■ Help the Melancholic Viking.
## Features
The Viking Longhouse features one main attraction.
### Melancholic Viking
The [[Melancholic Viking]] is an [[NPC]] located on the wooden throne at the end of the Viking Longhouse. They ask the player to remind them of the sea, requiring a fish to be cooked in the furnace, the fire to be put out and multiple splashes from the [[Fish Hat]]. After doing so, lightning will strike down from the sky and the NPC, now named [[Melancholic Viking|Joyful Viking]], will open their shop to the entire lobby for a few minutes.
## History

|May 4, 2022|Added Viking Longhouse.|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Revamped the Viking Longhouse, and expanded it to replace the [[Lonely Island]].|

Goldsworth III]] [[Marco]] [[Marina]] [[Paul]] [[Piggy]] [[Rabbit Bro]] [[Rabbit Cousin]] [[Rabbit Daddy]] [[Rabbit Dog]] [[Rabbit Granny]] [[Rabbit Security]] [[Rabbit Sis]] [[Rabbit Uncle]] [[Romero]] [[Salesman]] [[Scorpius]] [[Security Clerk]] [[Security Sloth]] [[Seymour]] [[Shen's Agent]] [[Susan]] [[Taylor]] [[The Handler]] [[Torrhus Canyon (NPC)]] [[Wool Weaver]] [[Zog]]

The Village is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Hub Island]] with many vendors and various [[NPC|NPCs]]. It is visited by the player when one first leaves their [[Private Island]] through the portal.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Village, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Village
■ Explore the area to learn more about SkyBlock.
■ Visit the Auction House.
■ Manage your coins in the Bank.
■ Enchant items in the Library.
## Features
The Village serves as a starting point for SkyBlock players. It features a variety of buildings, [[NPC|NPCs]], and branching off points to other [[Locations]] on the [[Hub Island]] and beyond.
### Buildings
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|Village|The spawn point and center area of the [[Hub Island]]. Splits off into other areas focused on specific [[Skills]].|[[Agnes]], [[Alcina]], [[Alixer]], [[Amelia]], [[Baker]], [[Bingo (NPC)]], [[Christopher]], [[Chuckleton]], [[Fear Mongerer]], [[Fisherwoman Enid]], [[Guy]], [[Hoppity]], [[Hub Selector]], [[Hypixel Discord]], [[Jerry]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Piggy]], [[Romero]], [[Salesman]], [[Torrhus Canyon (NPC)]]|
|^ [[Abiphones & Co.]]|Allows the player to purchase basic [[Abiphones]].|[[Alda]], [[Karis]]|
|^ [[Auction House]]|Allows the player to bid on and purchase items sold by other players.|[[Auction Agent]], [[Auction Master]]|
|^ [[Bank]]|Features the [[Banker]], who allows the player to store their [[Coins]].|[[Banker]]|
|^ [[Bazaar Alley]]|Allows the player to access the [[Bazaar]] to purchase commodity items.|[[Bazaar (NPC)]], [[Bazaar Agent]]|
|^ [[Builder's House]]|Features merchants who sell blocks for building.|[[Builder]], [[Mad Redstone Engineer]], [[Wool Weaver]]|
|^ [[Community Center]]|Features [[Elizabeth]]'s shop, [[City Projects]], and access to the [[Security Hall]] and [[Election Room]].|[[Aatrox]], [[Biblio]], [[Candidate Jerry]], [[Clerk Seraphine]], [[Cole]], [[Derpy]], [[Diana]], [[Diaz]], [[Elizabeth]], [[Finnegan]], [[Foxy]], [[Marina]], [[Paul]], [[Scorpius]], [[Susan]]|
|^ [[Security Hall]]|Contains a quest that teaches the player about account security.|[[Brigette]], [[Jimmy]], [[Security Clerk]], [[Security Sloth]]|
|^ [[Election Room]]|Allows the player to participate in Mayor Elections ([[Mayor Election]]).|[[Aatrox]], [[Candidate Jerry]], [[Clerk Seraphine]], [[Cole]], [[Derpy]], [[Diana]], [[Diaz]], [[Finnegan]], [[Foxy]], [[Marina]], [[Paul]], [[Scorpius]]|
|^ [[Fashion Shop]]|Allows the player to purchase Tuxedos ([[Tuxedo]]).|[[Seymour]]|
|^ [[Flower House]]|Features [[Marco]], who talks about [[Sprays]].|[[Marco]]|
|^ [[Canvas Room]]|Allows the player to paint images.|[[Marco]]|
|^ [[Museum]]|Serves as a place to store items in exchange for SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Curator]], [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]|
|^ [[Hexatorum]]|Contains [[The Handler]], who can upgrade the player's items.|[[The Handler]]|
|^ [[Pet Care]]|Features various Pet ([[Pets]])-related NPCs ([[NPC]]).|[[Bea]], [[Fann]], [[George]], [[Kat]], [[Zog]]|
|^ [[Rabbit House]]|Features a variety of [[Chocolate Factory]] employees.|[[Coach Jackrabbit]], [[Finance Rabbit]], [[Rabbit Bro]], [[Rabbit Cousin]], [[Rabbit Daddy]], [[Rabbit Dog]], [[Rabbit Granny]], [[Rabbit Security]], [[Rabbit Sis]], [[Rabbit Uncle]]|
|^ [[Sewer]]|Contains Rats ([[Rat]]).|None|
|^ [[Shen's Auction]]|Contains displays of items in the [[Auction House]], as well as exclusive items that players can bid on every SkyBlock Year ([[Time Systems]]).|[[Damia]]|
|^ [[Regalia Room]]|Requires the [[Shen's Regalia]] to be entered. Contains exclusive items that players can purchase.|[[Shen's Agent]]|
|^ [[Taylor's Shop]]|Allows the player to purchase Cosmetics from [[Taylor]].|[[Taylor]]|
### Map
The [[Map (Hub)|Hub Map]] is located in front of spawn. The map displays most islands with the exception of seasonal islands.
Hub World Map
### Portal to Private Island
The Portal to a player's [[Private Island]] is found in the center of the village.
### Adjacent Areas
The Village is connected to many other sections of the [[Hub Island]]. These locations include:
- The [[Forest]] to the left, which leads to [[The Park]].
- The [[Combat Settlement]] to the middle left, which leads to the [[Graveyard]] and [[Spider's Den]].
- The [[Mining District]] straight forward, which leads to the [[Gold Mine]].
- The [[Farm]] to the middle right, which leads to [[The Barn]].
- The [[Fishing Outpost]] to the right, which grants access to the [[Ship|Ship Navigator]] and various [[Ship|Fishing Islands]].
- The [[Wilderness]], [[Mountain]], [[Ruins]], and other areas.
### Conditional Builds
#### Events
During certain [[Events]], parts of the Village will have its build changed. For example, the [[Event Square]] can be found to the left of the Village, where a fountain and the portal to the [[Dungeon Hub]] is located. Events and their respective [[NPC|NPCs]] can be accessed here, such as:
- The [[Spooky Festival]] and the [[Fear Mongerer]]
- The [[Season of Jerry]] and [[Jerry]]
  - The [[New Year Celebration]] and the [[Baker]]
- The [[Traveling Zoo]] and [[Oringo]]
During other events, such as the [[Year of the Pig]], [[Year of the Witch|Witch]], or [[Year of the Seal|Seal]], a section between the [[Community Center]] and [[Museum]] will change to be themed around the respective event.
Similarly, when [[Simon]] appeared during the [[Raffle of the Century]], a section between the [[Community Center]] and [[Auction House]] had its build changed, to allow players to participate in the event.
#### Updates
When new [[SkyBlock]] updates are released, builds and [[NPC|NPCs]] are sometimes added to the Village to announce their addition.
|Update|Date|NPC|Location|
|-|-|-|-|
|0.25|May 26, 2026|Lotus Atoll ([[Lotus Atoll (NPC)]])|Next to [[Fisherwoman Enid]], towards the [[Fishing Outpost]].|
|0.27|August 4, 2026|Torrhus Canyon ([[Torrhus Canyon (NPC)]])|Next to [[Auction House]], towards the [[Foraging Camp]].|

## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Village Plaza will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Reverse-Murder Investigation
⇨ Fancy Sneakers
⇨ Barry, Premier Leader of SkyBlock
⇨ Cowboy Nick's Rabbits
⇨ Craft the Crux Relic
⇨ Shen Auctions
⇨ Enigma: Rabbit in the Hat!
And 9 more!
Enigma Souls: 7/7
## Purpose
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Village Plaza features many attractions. The player must have secured both the [[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]] and [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 8:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location.
### Montezuma Cat Soul
Located in [[Book in a Book]], a sub-location of the Village Plaza, the player can find the fifth [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma Soul Piece]], which is located at [[Coordinates|2, 63, -17]]. Finding the soul piece will add an additional +15 [[Rift Time]] to the [[Montezuma Pet|Pet]].

# Void Sepulture

ID: VOID_SEPULTURE
The Void Sepulture is a sub-location of [[The End]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Void Sepulture, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Void Sepulture
■ Defeat fanatics for Enderman Slayer.
■ Be careful.
## Features
The Void Sepulture features a few attractions.
### Teleport Pad
Located at [[Coordinates|-562, 7, -314]] is a teleport pad granting access to the Void Sepulture. The player must have at least [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] in order to use the teleport pad to enter the Void Sepulture. Above the teleport pad is a hologram reading DANGEROUS!.
## Trivia
- Players that somehow manage to enter the Void Sepulture without meeting the [[Combat]] requirement will be teleported out.
- Sepulture is an archaic word for a burial.

ID: VOID_SLATE
The Void Slate is a sub-location of [[The End]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Void Slate, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Void Slate
■ Perform Draconic Sacrifices.
■ Spend Dragon Essence.
## Features
The Void Slate features a few attractions.
### Tyzzo
[[Tyzzo]] is an [[NPC]] located in the Void Slate. They offer the player a guide to Draconic sacrifices, explaining the rewards from sacrificing certain items, including their rarity and amount. [[Tyzzo]] also has a [[Essence Shops|Essence Shop]] for [[Dragon Essence]], where the player can spend [[Essence]] on [[The End|End]]-related perks.
### Draconic Altar
Situated next to [[Tyzzo]] is the [[Draconic Altar]], where players can perform sacrifices. The player can offer an [[The End|End]]-related piece of gear to receive a variety of items and some [[Dragon Essence]].
## NPCs

# Walk of Fame
Aliases: Walk Of Fame
Categories: Locations
[Walk of Fame]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift Mountaintop ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: -223 19 115
← Previous Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Next Location →: [[Cerebral Citadel]] [[The Vents]] [[Wizardman Bureau]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Autonull (NPC)]] [[Blobbercyst (NPC)]] [[Lost Wizard]] [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth II]] [[Noway]] [[Obo]] [[Roosterzilla]] [[Spike (NPC)]]
[Metadata]
ID: WALK_OF_FAME
The Walk of Fame is a sub-location of [[The Mountaintop]]. It can be entered by speaking to the [[Argofay Doorholder]] if you've obtained the [[Supreme Timecharm]] and serves as the ultimate checklist for the Rift Dimension.
## Features
The Walk of Fame is split up into multiple rooms based on [[Rift Timecharms|Timecharms]]:
### Chicken N Egg Timecharm
The first room is based on the [[West Village]]. It requires a [[Warding Diamathame]] to trace a heart-shaped outline. Switching items or failing to trace the floating heart will result in the outline being reset.
Successfully tracing the heart summons [[Roosterzilla]], who requires the [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]] to continue.
### mrahcemiT esrevrorriM
The second room is based on the [[Mirrorverse]]. The player can see blocks on the other side of a mirror that shows where to jump. The [[Lost Wizard]] is at the end and requires the [[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]] to continue.
### SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm
The third room is based on the [[Village Plaza]]. The player has to walk past [[Temporal Pillar|Temporal Pillars]] to speak to [[Noway]], who requires the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]] to continue.
### Living Timecharm
The fourth room is based on the [[Living Stillness]]. The player has to use a [[Frozen Water Pungi]] and [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] to mine a living metal snake. Mining a snake summons an [[Autonull]] that requires the [[Living Timecharm]] to continue.
### Globulate Timecharm
The fifth room is based on the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]]. The player needs to hit several [[Globowl|Globowls]] into a giant [[Slime (disambiguation)|Slime]] named B in the center. After knocking several [[Globowl|Globowls]] into B, a [[Blobbercyst]] appears. It requires the [[Globulate Timecharm]] to continue.
### Vampiric Timecharm
The sixth room is based on the [[Stillgore Château]]. The player needs to break 2 [[Stillgore Château|Blood Effigies]]. Both must be broken within 1min 30s of each other before the first resets.
The first [[Stillgore Château|Blood Effigy]] can be reached by:
1. Jumping to the first platform to the right of the center platform
2. Turning right and jumping onto the platform where a tree is found
3. Climbing up using the ladder
4. Jumping across the branches and onto a floating island
The second [[Stillgore Château|Blood Effigy]] can be reached by:
1. Jumping to the first platform to the left of the center platform
2. Walking to the far left corner
3. Turn right and going up the ladder near the castle
4. Turn left and crossing into the room
5. Once in the room, go left and up the stairs
6. Jump until reaching the Effigy
Breaking both [[Stillgore Château|Blood Effigies]] summons [[Spike (NPC)|Spike]], who requires the [[Vampiric Timecharm]] to continue.
### Celestial Timecharm
The seventh room is based on [[The Mountaintop]]. [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth II]] requires the [[Celestial Timecharm]] to continue to the [[Wizardman Bureau]].
This room also connects to the [[Cerebral Citadel]]. It requires showing [[Shen's Ringalia]] to [[Obo]].

|-|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- The Walk of Fame is based off of [[Scoop]]'s house on the [[Mountain]].
- An [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] can be found in the seventh room behind a weak wall and hay. It can be broken with a [[Horsezooka]].

# West Village
Aliases: Hacking
Categories: Locations
[West Village]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Sub-locations: [[Around Colosseum]] [[Cake House]] [[Dolphin Trainer]] [[Infested House]]
Enigma Souls: 7
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
Coordinates: -80 70 -56
← Previous Location: [[Black Lagoon]]
Next Location →: [[Dreadfarm]] [[Mirrorverse]] [[Village Plaza]] Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]])
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Alchemist (Rift)]] [[Chef]] [[Cosmo]] [[Detective Amog]] [[Disinfestor]] [[Finplex]] [[Grandma]] [[Gunther]] [[Joey McPizza]] [[Kat (Rift)]] [[Master Tactician Fink]] [[Motes Grubber]] [[Plumber Joe (Rift)]] [[Skylark]] [[Sorcerer Okron]] [[Unhinged Kloon]] [[Yoshua]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Shadow]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Shadow Crux]]
Resources: [[Hot Dog]]
[Metadata]
ID: WEST_VILLAGE
The West Village is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Village]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the West Village will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Hacking for dummies
⇨ Gunther's Race
⇨ Complete the Mirrorverse
⇨ Ward against the fireball
⇨ Craft the Crux Ring
⇨ Cleanup the Infested House
⇨ Enigma: Hot Dog Contest
And 7 more!
Enigma Souls: 7/7
## Features
The West Village features many attractions. The player must have secured the [[Supreme Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 3:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location.
### Hacking
After talking to [[Unhinged Kloon]] in the cave near the [[Black Lagoon]], the player will receive a [[Retro-Encabulating Visor]]. Wearing this will reveal wires, which when followed, each lead to a different terminal. All the terminals must be hacked, and then must be set to rainbow colors when facing [[Unhinged Kloon]]. Once completed, [[Unhinged Kloon|Kloon]] will offer the player a [[Defective Monitor]] as a reward.
### Rift Race
The rift race is a short parkour course around the West Village. The race can be unlocked after talking to [[Gunther]], and can be started after running over the golden pressure plate. The player must run over all 10 checkpoints in order to complete the race. After completing the race under 60s, the player will receive a [[Dark Pebble]] from [[Gunther]], and a [[Gunthesizer Lichen]] after completing it in under 35s.
### Hot Dog Contest
In one of the buildings in the West Village is the [[Chef]]'s house, where both him and [[Joey McPizza]] reside.
Talking to the [[Chef]] will greet the player with beginning the Hot Dog Contest, a 60 second event where the Chef's furnace will rapidly spew out [[Hot Dog|Hot Dogs]]. Players must deposit a minimum of 50 [[Hot Dog|Hot Dogs]] on [[Joey McPizza]]'s plate to succeed in the event. Doing so will cause the [[Chef]] to reveal an enigma soul behind the furnace.
### Montezuma Cat Soul
Located on top of one of the buildings in the West Village, the player can find the fourth [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma Soul Piece]], which is located at [[Coordinates|-62, 88, -81]]. Finding the soul piece will add an additional +15 [[Rift Time]] to the [[Montezuma Pet|Pet]].
## NPCs
There are 17 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the West Village:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Alchemist (Rift)]]|[[Alchemist (Rift)]]|West Village; -49.5, 70, -65.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Gives out the [[Anti-Morph Potion]] in exchange for various items.|✔|
|[[Chef]]|[[Chef]]|West Village; -103, 72.0625, -104 ([[Coordinates]])|Hosts the Hot Dog Contest.|✕|

# Wilderness
Aliases: Fairy Pond, Fairy Tree, Fairy Trees, Romero's House
Categories: Locations
[Wilderness]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Dark Auction]]
Sub-locations: [[Artist's Abode]]
Fairy Souls: 11
Music: "Abstract Ringing ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: 106 66 118
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Gladiator]] [[Lucius]] [[Romero]] [[Shifty]] [[Sirius]] [[Tia the Fairy]] [[Vincent]]
[Metadata]
ID: WILDERNESS
The Wilderness is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Hub Island]]. It primarily features [[Tia the Fairy]] and the Fairy Pond, as well as the [[Dark Auction]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Wilderness, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Wilderness
■ Fish in the pond.
■ Visit Tia the Fairy at the Fairy Pond.
■ Discover hidden secrets.
## Features
The Wilderness features many attractions.
### Fairy Pond
The Fairy Pond is a pond located next to [[Tia the Fairy]]. A [[Fishing Hotspots|Fishing Hotspot]] may sometimes appear in that pond.
### Tavern
The Tavern is a building located next to the [[Dark Auction]]. It hosts several [[NPC|NPCs]]: [[Shifty]], who sells various [[Brews]]; [[Lucius]], who sells the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgrades to the player for a cost; and the [[Gladiator]].
### Dark Auction
Located in a house in the Wilderness, next to the Tavern is the [[Dark Auction]]. Every 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]], [[Sirius]] will appear outside the house, and offer entry to the [[Dark Auction|auction]]. The player must have at least 400,000 [[Coins]] in their purse to enter. During the event, [[Sirius]] will auction a set of exclusive items, which players can bid on and compete to win.
Behind the Dark Auction building, an [[Ender Chest]] is located that is used to move certain items in and out of [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
### Headless Horseman
The [[Headless Horseman]] can be spawned in the Wilderness using a [[Horseman's Candle]]. This can only be done at [[Time Systems|night]].
### Broken Cage
The Broken Cage is unofficial name of the portion featuring Soul Sand, near the Tavern. It has no use, but based on [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], [[The Wither King]] was imprisoned in it, until his escape which created [[Broken Cage]] in Rift Dimension's [[Wyld Woods]].
## NPCs
There are 7 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Wilderness:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gladiator]]|[[Gladiator]]|Wilderness; 123.5, 79, 165.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Used to run the [[Colosseum]].|✕|
|[[Lucius]]|[[Lucius]]|Wilderness; 125, 73, 165 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgrades after purchasing certain amounts of items from the [[Dark Auction]].|✔|
|[[Romero]]|[[Romero]]|Wilderness; 146.5, 112, 113.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the seventh step of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, and requires the player to have 1,291 [[Intelligence]].|✕|
|[[Shifty]]|[[Shifty]]|Wilderness; 114.5, 73, 175 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various [[Brews]].|✔|
|[[Sirius]]|[[Sirius]]|Wilderness; 91.5, 75, 176.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to sign up for the [[Dark Auction]].|✕|
|[[Tia the Fairy]]|[[Tia the Fairy]]|Wilderness; 119.5, 65, 147.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to [[Fairy Souls]], and allows them to exchange souls for rewards.|✕|
|[[Vincent]]|[[Vincent]]|[[Artist's Abode]]; 79.5, 74, 53.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to [[Dyes]], and lets players preview how each dye looks like.|✕|

The Windmill is a sub-location of [[The Barn]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Windmill, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Windmill
■ Talk to the Windmill Operator.
## Features
### Windmill
The Windmill itself is a structure and the main attraction of the area. It hosts the [[Windmill Operator]], and contains a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]]. It serves no purpose whatsoever.
### Windmill Operator
The [[Windmill Operator]] is an [[NPC]] located in the Windmill. They request 64 (1 stack) [[String]] and 64 [[Cobblestone]] respectively to "repair" the Windmill. After the player gives them the materials, the [[Windmill Operator]] will offer the player the [[Farm Merchant]]'s shop, with the exact same products sold.

# Wizard Tower
Aliases: Wizard Portal, Wizard's Tower
Categories: Locations
[Wizard Tower]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to the Wizard Tower]]
Fairy Souls: 5
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -47 119 93
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Elise]] [[Erihann]] [[Grumblefoot]] [[Nicole]] [[Udium]] [[Wizard]] [[Wizard's Assistant]]
[Metadata]
ID: WIZARD_TOWER
The Wizard Tower is a sub-location of the [[Hub Island]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Wizard Tower, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Wizard Tower
■ Talk to the Wizard.
■ Use the Wizard Portal
## Features
The Wizard Tower features a few attractions.
### Wizard Portal
Located in the center of the seventh and final floor of the Wizard Tower is the Wizard Portal. Entering the portal will immediately teleport the player next to it, prompting dialogue from the [[Wizard]].
Once the player is [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 12]] or higher, they can talk to the [[Wizard]] to grant exclusive dialogue. Once it has been completed, the player can purchase access to the [[Rift Dimension]] for a limited amount of time. Accessing the Portal once the player has purchased access will teleport the player to the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## NPCs
There are 7 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Wizard Tower:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Elise]]|[[Elise]]|Wizard Tower; 44.5, 77, 100.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Grants the player the [[Rift Necklace]], after donating the [[Supreme Timecharm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].|✕|
|[[Erihann]]|[[Erihann]]|Wizard Tower; 42.5, 97, 93.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to imbue their [[Rift Prism]] to obtain free entry to the [[Rift Dimension]].|✕|
|[[Grumblefoot]]|[[Grumblefoot]]|Wizard Tower; 44, 113, 99.5938 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Travel Scroll to the Wizard Tower]] for 11,111 [[Coins]].|✔|
|[[Nicole]]|[[Nicole]]|Wizard Tower; 41.5, 93, 96.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the Calamity ([[Jythar]]) and [[The Breach]].|✕|
|[[Udium]]|[[Udium]]|Wizard Tower; 46.5, 101, 100.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Returns items that the player has transferred to the [[Rift Dimension]] through bugs and exploits.|✕|
|[[Wizard]]|[[Wizard]]|Wizard Tower; 48.5, 119, 99.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access the [[Rift Dimension]] through the Wizard Portal ([[Wizard Tower]]).|✔|
|[[Wizard's Assistant]]|[[Wizard's Assistant]]|Wizard Tower; 48.5, 119, 99.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access the [[Rift Dimension]] through the Wizard Portal ([[Wizard Tower]]), replacing the [[Wizard]] after the player has closed [[The Breach]].|✔|

## Trivia
- Casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] into the Wizard Portal before the [[Rift Dimension]] was released would result in the following message: Sorry, no update here :(
- If you don't have a Dimensional Infusion purchased, the text above the Wizard Portal reads Travel to: Limbo ("Travel to: Limbo") rather than Travel to: The Rift.
- There are leftover [[Wool|Yellow Wool]] and [[Block of Lapis Lazuli|Lapis Lazuli]] blocks hidden within the tower's roof and underneath the tower (see Gallery). They, however, cannot be seen without using 3rd-party tools.

The Wizardman Bureau is a sub-location of [[The Mountaintop]]. It can be entered by completing the [[Walk of Fame]] and obtaining the [[Celestial Timecharm]].
## Features
The Wizardman Bureau features a few attractions.
### Wizardman Trials
After saying Help Wizardman! in chat, [[Wizardman]] offers the [[Wizardman|Wizardman Trials]].
### Connections
A back door exits back to [[The Mountaintop]] near [[The Breach]]. This can only be used after completing the [[Wizardman|Wizardman Trials]] and obtaining the [[Wizard Face]].
[[The Vents]] can also be accessed up a ladder on the left.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Wizardman Bureau:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Button]]|[[Button]]|Wizardman Bureau; -222.5, 19, -86.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Tells the player how to call [[Wizardman]] into the Wizardman Bureau.|✕|
|[[Wizardman]]|[[Wizardman]]|Wizardman Bureau; -222.5, 19, -88.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here after typing "Help Wizardman!" in chat, and lets the player earn various items that lead to becoming Wizardman.|✕|
## Trivia
- The Wizardman Bureau is based off of [[Scoop]]'s house on the [[Mountain]].
- A [[Button]] exists in the middle of the room, which gives dialogue when spoken to.

# Woods Race
Aliases: Wood Race, Woods Racing
Categories: Locations, Race
[Woods Race]
Woods Race.png The Woods Racing starting line
Type: Race ([[Island Racing]]) location
Location: [[Spruce Woods]]
Coordinates: -385 89 52
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Gustave]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Polished Pebble]] [[Hunter Knife]] [[Wolf Paw]] [[Silky Lichen]]
The Woods Race is a race in the [[Spruce Woods]] similar to the [[End Race]] in [[The End]]. [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] are disabled during a race.
The player can speak to [[Gustave]] to learn about the race and claim rewards.
## Rewards
|Time|Reward|Rarity|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|2 minutes|[[Polished Pebble]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Creates a spark when right-clicked|
|1 minute|[[Hunter Knife]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+50 [[Damage]] and +40 [[Speed]]|
|32 seconds|[[Wolf Paw]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+1 [[Speed]]|
|18 seconds|[[Silky Lichen]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Used to craft [[Silk-Edge Sword]]|
## Tips
- While [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] are disabled during the race, an [[Aspect of the End]] is not.
  - If the player cannot use an [[Aspect of the End]], then they may want to use a combination of a [[Grappling Hook]], [[Speed Potion|Speed IV Potion]], and [[Rogue Sword]].

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 11, 2019|0.7.4|Added Woods Race.|

ID: WYLD_WOODS
The Wyld Woods is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Wilderness]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Wyld Woods will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Help out Bughunter
⇨ Help out Inverted Sirius
⇨ Enigma: Tough Bark
⇨ Enigma: Woods Flower Pot
⇨ Enigma: Wither Cage
⇨ Enigma: Pressurized
⇨ Enigma: Two Plates
And 4 more!
Enigma Souls: 9/9
## Purpose
The Wyld Woods are full of a network of trees, full of [[NPC|NPCs]]. Walking along the trees are [[Argofay Branchstrutter|Argofay Branchstrutters]], which serve no purpose. [[Odonata|Odonatas]] can be found floating around the trees, and the player can use an [[Empty Odonata Bottle]] to convert the item into a [[Bottled Odonata]]. Additionally, [[Larva|Larvae]] can be found crawling across tree branches, and [[Larva Silk]] can be harvested from them using a [[Larva Hook]].

# Wyrmgrove Tomb

ID: WRYMGROVE_TOMB
The Wyrmgrove Tomb is a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Wyrmgrove Tomb is a large tomb composed of stone brick walls with several floors connected via staircases. [[Chillshot|Chillshots]] and [[Chillblade|Chillblades]] also spawn here.
### Tomb Floodway
The [[Tomb Floodway]] is primarily a [[Fishing]] location. No [[Sea Creatures]] can be caught while in the [[Tomb Floodway]]. Additionally, it grants a passive 10 [[Treasure Chance]] and is the only place [[Flexbone|Flexbones]] can be caught.
### Bubbleboost Column
The [[Bubbleboost Column]] is a shortcut back to [[Tangleburg]]. Teleporting down instead leads to the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]].

# Zealot Bruiser Hideout
Categories: Locations
[Zealot Bruiser Hideout]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XX (20) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Mythic Warfare ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])" (During an [[Ender Dragon]] fight)
Location: [[The End]]
← Previous Location: [[Void Slate]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Zealot Bruiser]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] [[Ender Pearl]] [[Pearlescent Dye]]
Resources: [[End Stone]] [[Obsidian]]
[Metadata]
ID: ZEALOT_BRUISER_HIDEOUT
The Zealot Bruiser Hideout is a sub-location of [[The End]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Zealot Bruiser Hideout, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Zealot Bruiser Hideout
■ Fight powerful Zealot Bruisers.
## Features
The Zealot Bruiser Hideout features [[Zealot Bruiser|Zealot Bruisers]], stronger version of [[Zealot|Zealots]] with higher health and damage. [[Zealot Bruiser|Zealot Bruisers]] have a higher odds of spawning a [[Special Zealot]] than the ones on the Dragon's Nest.
### Portal
There are two portals located in the [[Void Slate]], that both warp the player to the same location in the Zealot Bruiser Hideout. The player must have [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] in order to use the portals and access the zone.

# Dragon Queen
Aliases: Dragon Empress
Categories: Lore, Non-player characters
This page is about parts of the game's lore, its content may only appear in [[NPC]] dialogue, [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], item descriptions or similar sources.
[Dragon Queen]
Type: [[NPC]]
The Dragon Queen, sometimes called Dragon Empress, is the ruler of all dragons following the death of [[Jythar]].
## Story
After the death of [[Jythar]] during the [[Jythar|Calamity]], all summoned dragons worship the Dragon Queen, or Dragon Empress (translation unclear according to [[Corm]]). The [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragons]] are said to be the her direct spawns. It is possible that her and [[Jythar]] had a romantic connection.
## History

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Dragon Queen first mentioned by Corm.|
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added the Lone Adventurer whose dragon descriptions mention the Queen.|

# General Dan
Categories: Lore, Non-player characters
This page is about parts of the game's lore, its content may only appear in [[NPC]] dialogue, [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], item descriptions or similar sources.
[General Dan]
Type: [[NPC]]
General Dan was a general sent by a human king to lead the expedition into [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. He and most of the expedition's members died in the process.
## Story
- In the year [[Time Systems|1531 AE]], Zombies appeared near the sealed Catacombs, and due to this, a human king decided to launch an expedition there in fear of [[The Wither King]]. He put General Dan in charge of the expedition, and sent a mages from his personal guard, including [[Solomon]] and [[Sadan]], with him. The group of 50 soldiers and 3 mages, one of which kept a journal of their progress, were to leave the next morning. Halfway through second floor of the Catacombs, one of their scouts noticed an irregularity — a tomb that had seemingly been looted, without the trace of any remains. General Dan ordered the men to rest in the crypt.
- The soldiers were, however, attacked by a Zombie, who killed one soldier and wounded another. It was then killed by [[Solomon]]. The expedition continued an hour after this attack, until they found another undead, a Wither Skeleton, who single-handedly killed 3 soldiers. Solomon suggested ending the expedition and reporting back to the king, with his theory that [[The Wither King]] had survived and does, in fact, control the Catacombs now. General Dan decided to explore the third floor, and then go back.
- At the end of the third floor, the humans encountered a large room with water pipes, and they took a break there and prepared their lunch. One of the soldiers yelled that enemies are coming, and they were quickly surrounded by hundreds of Wither Skeletons, Giant Spiders, Zombies and Skeletons. Dan rallied the soldiers to a desperate fight, assisted by [[Solomon]].
- In another journal kept by one of the soldiers, the author describes [[Solomon]]'s and Dan's bravery and how one of the mages, the one keeping the report, took an opportunity to escape. The mage stunned a group of enemies and made a run for it, followed by several soldiers. However, the escaping mage did not survive, and the last volume of their journal was purposely damaged. Meanwhile, another Wither Skeleton entered the room, attacking Dan directly. Using his two-handed sword, he cut the Skeleton in half and its sword with it.
- Soon after, however, [[Solomon]] sensed something and warned everyone that they need to leave. A [[Necron|Wither]] entered the room and killed the General with a single spell cast. The rest of the soldiers ran in disarray, while [[Solomon]] cast a barrier in an attempt to cover them.
## Trivia
- The sword he used could be, based on the sword's description, a [[Dark Claymore]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Journal Entries, which mention Dan and the expedition.|
Linked from: [[Mentioned Entities]], [[Sadan]], [[Solomon]], [[Time Systems]]

# Jythar
Aliases: Calamity, Cataclysm, Dragon Emperor, The Calamity, The Eternal One
Categories: Lore
This page is about parts of the game's lore, its content may only appear in [[NPC]] dialogue, [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], item descriptions or similar sources.
[Jythar]
The skeleton of Jythar located in [[The Bastion]]
Jythar, The Dragon Emperor, was a [[Ender Dragon|Dragon]] that lead the attack on the Human Realm in the year 1111 AE. This attack is known as the [[Jythar|Calamity]]. He is not interactable in game, but a few NPCs and a dungeon journal talk about him, and his skeleton lies on the [[Crimson Isle]] in [[The Bastion]].
## Lore
### Story
- A long time ago, in the year 1111 AE, the millennia-old dragon ruler, Jythar, launched an attack with his entire Dragon army. Many kingdoms were obliterated by this attack, standing no chance against the massive Dragon army and their weird, unseen magic. During the attack, Jythar landed atop the [[Mountain]], triggering the trap that human mages, including [[The Wither King|Kaeman]], had set.
- The magic trap unleashed magical energy, immobilizing and crushing Jythar with the force of gravity. Dragon soldiers shielded the human attacks on their powerless emperor, but to no avail. However, defying all known laws of magic, Jythar slowly rose to his feet. The unusual aura of the passed on dragon was Fel, the magic of the dead, as if the dragon had made a pact with magic itself; he sacrificed his own soul for a brief moment of power and freedom. Jythar screamed, and for a moment time seemed to stand still, then a blast of dark energy has burst from the behemoth, freeing him from his trap and destroying everything in its way, killing thousands, sparing nobody, including his own soldiers and even a wing of his general Chozon.
- As the dragon's scream ripped through the air, time itself paused. At that moment, [[The Breach]] to the [[Rift Dimension]] opened at the peak of the [[Mountain]]. As Jythar’s pact with death came close, he fell to the earth, motionless but consumed by dark flames. The dragon's goal was achieved, leaving the realm in shambles. The familiar terrain had crumbled, shattered into a multitude of isolated islands. Jythar's heart, the core of the Fel magic, was placed somewhere in [[The End]].
- Against the will of the Mage Council, [[The Wither King|Kaeman]] set up a laboratory to study the heart closer, in an attempt to discover the secret of life and death to save his family, Sarah and Mylah, who had died during the Calamity.
### Aftermath
The immediate aftermath of Jythar's attacks involved the separation of the [[Locations|SkyBlock islands]], which was once a connected piece of terrain; [[The End]] becoming corrupted along with the creation of the [[Enderman|Endermen]]; and Kaeman becoming [[The Wither King]]. Jythar's remains, apart from his heart, are located in and near [[The Bastion]], whilst the specific location of his heart is unknown. Chozon's corpse on the other hand, is located in the [[Spider's Den]]. The other 3 generals, Dogen, Kirva, and Daydro perished and fell in the void according to [[Corm]]. After his death, the currently unnamed [[Dragon Queen|Dragon Empress]], who is implied to be in a romantic relationship with Jythar, became the new Dragon leader.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Jythar is first mentioned by [[Corm]] and [[Elle]].|
|Feb 16, 2023|Added the Dungeon Journals ([[Journal]]), including Memories.|
