# Shen
Categories: Lore, Non-player characters
This page is about parts of the game's lore, its content may only appear in [[NPC]] dialogue, [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], item descriptions or similar sources.
[Shen]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shen is an [[NPC]] running both [[Shen's Auction]] and its [[Barrier Street|Rift version]]. It is unclear if he has traveled to the [[Rift Dimension]] or only his counterparts.
There are 5 Shens in the [[Cerebral Citadel]]:
- [[Shen Chaos]]
- [[Shen Albedo]]
- [[Shen Citrinitas]]
- [[Shen Rubedo]]
- [[Shen Magnum Opus]]
## Lore
- According to [[Damia (Rift)|Damia]], one of the constants in each universe is that Shen is the richest person.
- According to [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]], Wizards believe that Shen employed or created [[Dante]].
  - Additionally from her dialogue, she suggests the idea that Shen may have discovered a way in the [[Rift Dimension]] before the wizards and found [[Dante]].
- According to the [[Goons]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was mayor, both [[Aura]] and [[Dante]] were part of Shen's team and left, with [[Dante]] leaving first and [[Aura]] following after.
## Trivia
- Shen could be associated with wizards due to [[Shen's Auction]] selling the [[Wizard Wand]], which is said to be a private joke amongst Wizards.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Added the [[Shen's Auction]], featuring the first mentions of Shen.|
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added the [[Rift Dimension]] alongside Damia ([[Damia (Rift)]]) and Elise ([[Elise (Rift)]]) providing additional information about him.|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added [[Shen Chaos]], Albedo ([[Shen Albedo]]), Citrinitas ([[Shen Citrinitas]]), Rubedo ([[Shen Rubedo]]), and Magnum Opus ([[Shen Magnum Opus]]) in the [[Cerebral Citadel]].|
|Nov 28, 2025|Re-added [[Goons]] to the game as part of [[Candidate Aura]]'s mayoral reign, featuring some dialogue about Shen.|

# Mythological Ritual
Aliases: Mythological Event
Categories: Mayor Election
[Mythological Ritual]
The Mythological Ritual is an [[Events|Event]] that occurs when [[Diana]] is elected as Mayor with her Mythological Ritual perk, or when [[Candidate Jerry]] is elected with his Perkpocalypse perk rotated to perks from Diana.
During the event, players can equip a [[Griffin Pet]] and use an [[Ancestral Spade]], [[Archaic Spade]], or [[Deific Spade]] to dig up [[Griffin Burrows]] in order to obtain rare items, spawn [[Mythological]] creatures for rare drops, and obtain [[Coins]].
## Griffin Burrows
Griffin Burrows are chains of burrows found around the [[Hub Island|Hub]], which can be discovered using the [[Ancestral Spade]] and its upgrades' Echo ability.
Burrows in a Chain:
|No. of Burrows|Source|
|-|-|
|4|[[Ancestral Spade]]|
|6|[[Archaic Spade]]|
|8|[[Deific Spade]]|
|+2|Erudite ([[Reforging]]) reforge from [[Daedalus' Notes]]|
|Σmax= 10||
All burrows have a 75% chance to spawn a [[Mythological]] creature, and a 25% chance to drop a treasure.
### Burrow Treasure
The treasure available from Griffin Burrows depends on which tier of Spade you are using to excavate them.

### Mythological Creatures
Occasionally, a [[Mythological]] creature will spawn from a Griffin Burrow. Increasing the rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]] will unlock 2 mobs per tier.
These will drop:
- ( [[Enchanted Ancient Claw|Enchanted]]) [[Ancient Claw|Ancient Claws]], [[Enchanted Gold Ingot|Enchanted Gold Ingots]], [[Mythological Dye]]
- their signature drop;
- and, for some of them, an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] which can be obtained through [[Hunting]] or the usage of [[Salts]].
|Name| [[Griffin Pet]] rarity|Weight|Main drop(s)|Drop chance|Shard|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minos Hunter]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1,000|[[Hilt of Revelations]]|0.25% → 2%|[[Minos Hunter Shard]] (Mythological Ruler ([[Attributes]]))|
|[[Siamese Lynxes]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1,000|[[Crochet Tiger Plushie]]|0.05% → 0.4%||
|[[Stranded Nymph]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|800|[[Washed-up Souvenir]]|0.2% → 0.5%||
|[[Cretan Bull]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|800|[[Cretan Urn]]|0.2% → 0.5%|[[Cretan Bull Shard]] (Mythological Resistance ([[Attributes]]))|
|[[Harpy]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|600|[[Antique Remedies]]|0.35% → 0.6%|[[Harpy Shard]] (Tree Lurker ([[Attributes]]))|
|[[Gaia Construct]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|600|[[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]]|0.35% → 0.6%||
|[[Minotaur]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|400|[[Daedalus Stick]]|0.04% → 0.08%|[[Minotaur Shard]] (Mythological Fortune ([[Attributes]]))|
|[[Minos Champion]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|400|[[Minos Relic]]|0.02% → 0.04%||
|[[Sphinx]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|75|[[Brain Food]]|0.25% → 0.5%|[[Sphinx Shard]] (Mythological Tracker ([[Attributes]]))|
|[[Minos Inquisitor]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|75|Chimera I ([[Chimera]])|1% → 1.25%||
|[[Manticore]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|15|[[Fateful Stinger]] [[Manti-core]]|0.5%; 0.2%||
|[[King Minos]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|15|[[Crown of Greed]] [[Shimmering Wool]]|2%; 0.2%|[[King Minos Shard]] (King of Greed ([[Attributes]]))|
The rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]] determines the summoned mob's prefix. One can upgrade their [[Griffin Pet]] using a [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]] at [[Kat]].
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] → Blessed
- [[Rarity|RARE]] → Stalwart
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] → Venerable
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] → Exalted
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] → Empyrean

 param     = tracking|Tracking|0|int|0-

|[[Hephaestus Remedies]]|[[Hephaestus Remedies]]|Increases the base [[Strength]] of your currently active pet by 100%.|Crafted with [[Hephaestus Anvil]] and [[Antique Remedies]]|
|[[Hephaestus Urn]]|[[Hephaestus Urn]]|Grants +10 [[Pet Luck]].|Crafted with [[Hephaestus Anvil]] and [[Cretan Urn]]|
|[[Hephaestus Plushie]]|[[Hephaestus Plushie]]|Grants +50 [[Attack Speed]].|Crafted with [[Hephaestus Anvil]] and [[Crochet Tiger Plushie]]|

|[[Hephaestus Souvenir]]|[[Hephaestus Souvenir]]|Grants +10 [[Sea Creature Chance]].|Crafted with [[Hephaestus Anvil]] and [[Washed-up Souvenir]]|
## Tracking
[[Tracking]] is an important stat in many aspects of SkyBlock, but especially the Mythological Ritual: it increases the chance of [[Elusive]] mobs spawning, which includes the [[Minos Inquisitor]], [[Sphinx]], [[Manticore]], and [[King Minos]].
The following items obtained from the Mythological Ritual grant [[Tracking]]:
- [[Brain Food]]: +5 [[Tracking]]
- [[Golden Fleece]]: +15 [[Tracking]] on [[Griffin Burrows]]
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Griffin Pet]] (Ancient Earth): +1-10 [[Tracking]] on [[Griffin Burrows]]
- [[Sphinx Shard]] ([[Attributes|Mythological Tracker]]): +5 [[Tracking]] on [[Griffin Burrows]]

# Accessory Power
Aliases: AP, MP, Magic Power, Magical Power
Categories: Mechanics
[Accessory Power]
Accessory Power (formerly known as Magical Power) is a mechanic that boosts the stats granted by [[Powers]] and grants Tuning Points.
## Mechanics
Each [[Accessories|Accessory]] the player has in their [[Accessory Bag]] gives an amount of Accessory Power based on its [[Rarity]]. The amount of Accessory Power the player has determines the player's Accessory Power multiplier, which is calculated with this formula:
  Stats Multiplier=29.97×(ln⁡(0.0019×(Accessory Power)+1))1.2
The stats given by the player's Accessory Power are multiplied by this multiplier. Dungeon Accessories give double Accessory Power inside [[Dungeons]].
|Rarity|Accessory Power|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|3|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|8|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|12|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|16|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|22|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|28|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|3|
|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|5|
|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|1|
|ADMIN ([[Rarity]])|22|
There are no [[Rarity|DIVINE]] or [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] accessories obtainable without glitches (besides the [[Celestial Starstone]] which is a [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]-exclusive accessory and does not grant Accessory Power), but they are listed for completeness.
For each Accessory Power the player has, they earn [[SkyBlock Levels|1 SkyBlock XP]].

If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Notes
- Dungeon [[Accessories]] grant twice as much Accessory Power as they normally would while inside [[Dungeons]].
## Trivia
- There are, as of SkyBlock 0.27.2, 151 Accessories in the game (excluding their own upgrades) and the maximum Accessory Power is 2,184 Accessory Power and another 252 Accessory Power from Dungeon Accessories when in dungeons, adding up to 2,436 Accessory Power in [[Dungeons]]. On top of that, 68 Accessories can be [[Enrichments|Enriched]].
- The only [[Accessories]] that give more Accessory Power than normal are the [[Hegemony Artifact]], which gives double Accessory Power, the [[Rift Prism]] which grants 11 Accessory Power when imbued at [[Erihann]], and the [[Abicases]], which grant 1 Accessory Power for every 2 contacts the player has.

# Auto-pickup
Aliases: Auto-Pickup, Telekinesis
Categories: Mechanics
[Auto-pickup]
Auto-pickup is a feature unlocked at [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 6]]. It causes all item and mob drops to directly be added to the inventory, and the [[Stash]] if there is no space in the inventory.
## Overview
Auto-pickup causes all item, mob, and block drops to be added directly to the [[Player|inventory]] without being dropped as physical items on the ground. The player can still drop items from their hotbar and inventory.
Some items are not affected by Auto-pickup, such as drops from [[Minibosses|Slayer Minibosses]]. This is most likely a bug.
## Game Message
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Auto-pickup triggers|AUTO-PICKUP! Drop sent to your inventory! [I GET IT]|
## Trivia
- Originally, the [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] could be applied to items at [[Rusty]] and served the same function as Auto-pickup.
  - After [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] was removed, all [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]]) became glitched [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] with no enchantment.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 25, 2022|Added Auto-pickup.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Now requires SkyBlock Level 6 ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) instead of Combat VI (6) ([[Combat]]).|
|Jul 1, 2026|Auto-pickup has been enabled on Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]]) and Slayers ([[Slayer]]). If they cannot be picked up, they are instead redirected into the [[Stash]].|
Linked from: [[Bingo]], [[Bone]], [[Changelog/2020/August 19]], [[Emperor's Skull]], [[Enchantments]], [[Flint Arrow]], [[Flint Skeleton]], [[Rusty]], [[Skeleton (Unincorporated)]], [[SkyBlock Levels/Rewards]], [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)]]

# Cracked Wall
Aliases: Weak Wall, Weak wall
Categories: Mechanics
[Cracked Wall]
Cracked Walls are destructible walls found around SkyBlock that can be destroyed using [[Superboom TNT]] or [[InfiniBoom™ TNT]]. They are made of Cracked Stone Bricks and usually serve as a barrier to a separate zone.
## Locations
|Zone|Leads to|
|-|-|
|[[Coal Mine]]|[[Crypts]]|
|[[Dark Thicket]]|[[Howling Cave]]|
|[[Savanna Woodland]]|[[Soul Cave]]|
|[[Jungle Island]]|[[Spirit Cave]]|
|[[Gunpowder Mines]]|Leads to a Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]])|
|[[Gravel Mines]]|[[Arachne's Burrow]]|
|[[Catacombs]]|Leads to several Secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]])|
|[[Dreadfarm]]|Leads to a Montezuma Soul Piece ([[Montezuma Pet]])|
|[[Living Cave]]|Leads to a Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]])|

- Cracked Walls can be destroyed with the [[Explosive Bow]].

# Damage Reduction
Categories: Mechanics, Pages with math equations
Damage Reduction is a mechanic closely related to [[Defense]] and [[True Defense]], which reduce the damage taken by the player. [[Defense]] reduces normal damage, and [[True Defense]] reduces [[True Damage]].
## Calculation
Both normal and true damage reduction are calculated using the same formula:
  DefDef+100,
where:
  Def - player's [[Defense]] or [[True Defense]]
Chart showing damage reduction.
[[Defense]] and damage reduction are inversely associated. This means, that the more [[Defense]] the player has, the less damage reduction increases. This leads to a misconception that higher [[Defense]] is useless because the damage reduction only goes up slightly. This is incorrect, for as the difference from 0% to 50% damage reduction may seem more substantial than the jump from 90% to 95%, both are equal in that they double [[Effective Health|EHP]] (100-200 vs 1000–2000).
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Added Damage Reduction.|
Linked from: [[Damage Calculation]]

# Gemstone Slot
Aliases: Gemstone Slots, Gemstone Stats
Categories: Mechanics
Gemstone Slots appear on items that are upgradable with [[Gemstone|Gemstones]]. One Gemstone can be applied to each Gemstone Slot. A stat bonus will be granted according to the Gemstone applied.
## Slot Types
Gemstone Slot types determine what types of Gemstones can be applied. The Stat Bonuses given by a Gemstone Slot are not determined by the slot type, but the Gemstone applied to it.
### Ordinary Gemstone Slots
Ordinary Gemstone Slots only allow for the corresponding [[Gemstone]] to be applied.
|Symbol|Name|Gemstone|Stat|
|-|-|-|-|
|[]|Ruby Slot|Ruby|[[Health]]|
|[]|Amethyst Slot|Amethyst|[[Defense]]|
|[]|Jade Slot|Jade|[[Mining Fortune]]|
|[]|Sapphire Slot|Sapphire|[[Intelligence]]|
|[]|Amber Slot|Amber|[[Mining Speed]]|
|[]|Topaz Slot|Topaz|[[Pristine]]|
|[]|Jasper Slot|Jasper|[[Strength]]|
|[]|Opal Slot|Opal|[[True Defense]]|
|[]|Onyx Slot|Onyx|[[Crit Damage]]|
|[]|Aquamarine Slot|Aquamarine|[[Fishing Speed]]|
|[]|Citrine Slot|Citrine|[[Foraging Fortune]]|
|[]|Peridot Slot|Peridot|[[Farming Fortune]]|
### Category Gemstone Slots
Category Gemstone Slots allow for certain types of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to be applied.
|Symbol|Name|Valid Gemstones Name|Valid Gemstones Stat|
|-|-|-|-|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Ruby|[[Health]]|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Amethyst|[[Defense]]|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Sapphire|[[Intelligence]]|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Jasper|[[Strength]]|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Onyx|[[Crit Damage]]|
|[⚔]|⚔ Combat Slot|Opal|[[True Defense]]|
|[☤]|☤ Defensive Slot|Ruby|[[Health]]|
|[☤]|☤ Defensive Slot|Amethyst|[[Defense]]|
|[☤]|☤ Defensive Slot|Opal|[[True Defense]]|
|[✦]|✦ Mining Slot|Jade|[[Mining Fortune]]|
|[✦]|✦ Mining Slot|Amber|[[Mining Speed]]|
|[✦]|✦ Mining Slot|Topaz|[[Pristine]]|
|[❂]|❂ Universal Slot|Any|Any|
### Chisel Gemstone Slots
Chisel Gemstone Slots allow for certain types of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to be applied to [[Chisels]], which grant perks when used in the [[Fossil Excavator]].
Unlike with all other items, the potency of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] is only affected by their tier, and not by rarity of the [[Chisel]].
Applying the same Gemstone multiple times to the same chisel increases the chance for it to trigger additively, for example applying 3 [[Gemstone|Flawless Aquamarine Gemstones]] will grant 180% chance to gain extra Charges, or 100% chance to gain +1 and 80% to gain +2 instead. It does NOT grant 60% chance to get +3 Charges nor does it rolls the 60% chance 3 times.
|Symbol|Name|Valid Gemstones Gemstone|Valid Gemstones Corresponding Perk|
|-|-|-|-|
|[❥]|❥ Chisel Slot|Aquamarine|Granting additional Chisel ([[Chisels]]) charges|
|[❥]|❥ Chisel Slot|Citrine|Highlighting treasure|
|[❥]|❥ Chisel Slot|Onyx|Spawning additional treasure|
|[❥]|❥ Chisel Slot|Peridot|Granting more [[Fossil Essence]]|
## Format
A [[Armor of Divan|Helmet Of Divan]] with 2 locked Amber slots, 2 Jade slots with Flawless Jade, and 1 Topaz slot with Perfect Topaz.
A [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Helmet]] with both slots unlocked. Note the slight color difference between locked and unlocked slots.
  Note: The circle ◯ is used to indicate any icon symbol that represents a Slot Type
A locked (and hence unequipped) Gemstone Slot looks like this: [◯] (Icon/Brackets: Dark Gray) An unlocked and unequipped Gemstone Slot looks like this: [◯] (Icon: Gray; Brackets: Dark Gray)
Each equipped Gemstone Slot displays 3 information:
1. The Icon Symbol shows the Gemstone Slot Type (see "Symbol" on the Slot Type table above)
2. The Icon Color shows the type of the applied Gemstone (see table below)
3. The Bracket Color shows the tier of the applied Gemstone (see table below)

## Applying and Removing
### Applying Gemstones
Gemstones can be applied to selected items at the gemstone grinder located near [[Geo]]. The Cost to Unlock (if any) must be paid in order to unlock the slot and apply the gemstone. The apply process is otherwise free of charge.
### Removing Gemstones
The applied gemstone can each be separately removed from any items at the gemstone grinder located near [[Geo]]. There is a confirmation menu to prevent accidentally removing them.
|Type|Cost to remove|
|-|-|
|Rough|1 Coin ([[Coins]])|
|Flawed|100 [[Coins]]|
|Fine|10,000 [[Coins]]|
|Flawless|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|Perfect|500,000 [[Coins]]|

# Guests Management
Categories: Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
Guests Management is an option in a [[SkyBlock Menu]] that is only accessible on your [[Private Island]].
## General information
Guests Management takes the appearance of [[Long Grass|Grass]] and can only be opened if someone is visiting your [[Private Island]]. It is located one slot away from the bottom left corner of [[SkyBlock Menu]].
It contains information about all [[Player|players]] currently visiting your [[Private Island]], including their Hypixel Level, Achievement Points, and time player spent visiting your [[Private Island]].
It can also be used to kick [[Player|players]] from your [[Private Island]].
## Descriptions
### Guests Management
Manage players guesting on your island.
Guests:
Click to manage!
### Player
Hypixel Level:
Achievement Points:
Guesting for:
Click to interact!
### Kick player
Get rid of this guest from your island.
Use /ignore add [playername] to ban them from visiting your island!
Command: /sbkick
Click to kick!
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Added Guests Management.|
Linked from: [[Changelog/2020/November 12]]

# Island Points
Categories: Mechanics
Player receives Island Points for finishing the Island Parkour
Island Points are points exclusive to [[Private Island|Private Islands]]. Island guests have the ability to earn Island Points for doing certain actions. These points are purely cosmetic, they serve no purpose nor give any advantage.
## Points
The following actions give guests Island Points:
- Guesting, a player receives +3 Island Point for each minute spent on the Island.
- [[Egg Hunt]], finding an Easter Egg gives +10 Island Points.
- [[Social|Island Parkour]], finishing an Island Parkour gives +5 Island Points.
## Trivia
- Island Points have no effect on [[Social|Social Skill]].
- People with the most Island Points appear on the [[Social Display]] of the Private Island.
- Island Points reset every week.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Island Points.|
Linked from: [[Display (disambiguation)]], [[Egg Hunt]], [[Island (disambiguation)]], [[Social Display]]

# Launch Pad (Structure)
Aliases: Launch Pad (structure)
Categories: Mechanics, Portals
[Launch Pad (Structure)]
Launch Pads are structures found at the edges of public [[Locations|Islands]], launching the player between areas.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Using a Launch Pad|Sending to server <server>...|
|Failing to use a Launch Pad|Unable to use this launch pad at the moment, please try again later. (PLAYER_TRANSFER_COOLDOWN)|
## Gallery
- Launch Pad from Hub Island to The Park Launch Pad from [[Hub Island]] to [[The Park]]
- Launch Pad from Hub Island to Spider's Den Launch Pad from [[Hub Island]] to [[Spider's Den]]
- Launch Pad from Hub Island to Gold Mine Launch Pad from [[Hub Island]] to [[Gold Mine]]
- Launch Pad from Hub Island to The Farming Islands Launch Pad from [[Hub Island]] to [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|The Farming Islands]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Launch Pad.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Now say "Travel to" instead of "Warp to".|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|The spawn of most Launch Pads are now a couple of blocks further away from them to prevent players from accidentally using them.|

# Mob Modifiers
Categories: Mechanics, Dungeon Mobs, Mobs
Mobs excluding [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]], [[Bosses|BOSS]] and their special minions can spawn with Modifiers.
## Corrupted Modifier
[[Sea Creatures]] can be corrupted using a [[Totem of Corruption]] or by touching a [[Sulphur]] block. Mobs on the [[Crimson Isle]] can also rarely spawn already corrupted.
Corrupted Mobs deal 2x [[Damage]]. They also drop [[Corrupted Fragment|Corrupted Fragments]] and [[Sulphur]]. For Sea Creatures they can also drop [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated 1]] trophy fish.
## Runic Modifier
Most mobs can spawn with Runic Modifier.
Most Runic Mobs have 4x [[Health]] and deal more damage. They also drop [[Runes]].
## Dungeon Mobs Modifiers
Dungeon Mobs has a chance to spawn with:
- Flaming: Mob's melee attacks set the victim on fire. (cannot be negated by [[Fire Talisman]] or [[Fire Resistance Potion|Fire Resistance]] potion effect)
- Stormy: Strikes lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
- Speedy: Runs faster.
- Fortified: Higher [[Defense]] and knockback resistance.
- Healthy: Higher maximum [[Health]]. On master mode these mobs have 0.4% chance to drop [[Vitamin Death]].
- Healing: Mob will slowly regenerate [[Health]] at all times.
## The Watcher Undeads Modifiers
Undeads spawned by The Watcher on [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Catacombs F5]] or above can spawn with:
- Stormy: Strikes lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].
- Boomer: Spawns a primed TNT on death, drops [[Superboom TNT]] on explode.
- Golden: Drops [[Gold Essence]] on death.
- Stealthy: Having [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]].
- Healthy: Higher maximum [[Health]] , 5% chance to drop [[Healing Tissue]] on death.
- Speedy: Runs faster, high chance to drop a [[Dungeon Potion]] (Same floor, e.g. F5 drops F5 Potion) with 1 higher [[Speed Potion|Speed]] tier on death.

|Unknown Date|Added Mob Modifiers.|

# Rarity
Aliases: Common, Divine, Legenjerry, Rare, Rarities, Special, Supreme, Ultimate, Uncommon, Valuable Items, Valuable items, Very Special
Categories: Mechanics
Rarity is one of the factors of any item in SkyBlock. An item's rarity serves as a gauge of its worth and usability. Rarity also affects how strong and costly a [[Reforging|Reforge]] will be. Every item in SkyBlock has a rarity associated with it.
## Levels
### Normal Rarities
|Rarity|Examples|Description / Properties|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON|[[Seeds]] [[Undead Sword]] [[Inflatable Jerry]] [[Handy Blood Chalice]]|Cannot have Auction starting price or BIN price set to higher than 5 times its NPC sell value by default.|
|UNCOMMON|[[Enchanted Coal]] [[Diamond Pickaxe]] [[Voidwalker Katana]] [[Jungle Amulet]]||
|RARE|[[Enchanted Diamond Block]] [[Pig's Foot]] [[Aspect of the End]] [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]||
|EPIC|[[Ember Rod]] Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) [[Jerry-chine Gun]] [[Overflux Power Orb]]||
|LEGENDARY|[[Aspect of the Dragons]] [[Dungeon Orb]] [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] [[Terminator]]||
|MYTHIC|[[Divan's Drill]] [[Cake Soul]] [[Plasma Bucket]]|Typically obtained by applying [[Recombobulator 3000]] to a LEGENDARY item.|
|DIVINE|[[Celestial Starstone]]|A rarity "kept exclusive to items of the caliber of the Divan’s Drill". Obtained by using a [[Recombobulator 3000]] on MYTHIC items. Has higher reforge stats than MYTHIC, but only has reforge stats for mining reforges. Formerly known as SUPREME prior to the one game patch.|
|SPECIAL|[[Game Breaker]] [[Pioneer Pickaxe]] [[New Year Cake]] Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]])|Generally trophy or cosmetic items. Has the same reforge stats as MYTHIC, but increased reforge ability potency for those which scale with rarity. SPECIAL accessories give same accessory power as COMMON ones, but can also be buffed with [[Enrichments]].|
|VERY SPECIAL|[[Game Annihilator]]|Obtained by applying [[Recombobulator 3000]] to SPECIAL or ULTIMATE items. Has the same reforge stats as MYTHIC, but increased reforge ability potency for those which scale with rarity. VERY SPECIAL accessories give same [[Accessory Power]] as UNCOMMON ones, but can also be buffed with [[Enrichments]].|

|Rarity|Examples|Description / Properties|
|-|-|-|
|ULTIMATE|[[Caduceus Mender Skin]]|This rarity is used for skins that can change appearance.|
|ADMIN|Titan's Helmet ([[Titan's Armor]]) Titan's Chestplate ([[Titan's Armor]]) Titan's Leggings ([[Titan's Armor]]) Titan's Boots ([[Titan's Armor]])|This rarity is exclusively for some of the [[Admin-only]] items.|
## Effects across SkyBlock
Rarity has a major effect on various elements across SkyBlock, from item modifiers to pets and [[Accessories]].
### Reforging and Gemstones
In SkyBlock, [[Weapons]], [[Armor]], and [[Tools]] (except [[Shovels]]) can be [[Reforging|Reforged]] to be gain extra stat bonuses. On top of that some but not all: [[Swords]], [[Armor]], [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], [[Axes]] and [[Fishing Rods]], as well as [[Sulphur Bow]], [[Pendant of Divan]] and [[Talisman of Power]] as well as its upgrades, can have Gemstones applied to gain additional stats. The higher the rarity of an item is, the greater the stat boost from both Reforges and Gemstones will be. The rarity will also determine the reforge price.
### Pet Rarity
Each [[Pets|Pet]] has its own rarity. The higher their rarity, the more abilities and stat boosts they grant the player, it also grants more [[Pets|Pet Score]].
### Accessories
Each Accessory grants [[Accessory Power]] based on the rarity of the item, from COMMON granting 3 [[Accessory Power]] to MYTHIC granting 22 [[Accessory Power]], SPECIAL and VERY SPECIAL grant the same [[Accessory Power]] as COMMON and UNCOMMON respectively.
## Trivia
- The Rarity Color of SPECIAL was changed from Pink to Red most likely because the MYTHIC rarity color was determined to be Pink in the Alpha Hypixel Network.
- Most LEGENDARY items, specifically weapons, have a unique UUID, preventing them from stacking and help determine possible duped items.
- Since the SPECIAL rarity supercedes the DIVINE rarity, recombobulating a DIVINE item will cause it to become SPECIAL. This can be seen by using [[Centauri|Centauri's]] Max Items feature in the [[Rift Dimension]] on a [[Celestial Starstone]].
- [[Jerry Barn Skin]] has the rarity "LEGENJERRY" which is fundamentally the same as LEGENDARY.
- [[Celestial Starstone]] is the only item that has DIVINE rarity by default.
  - All other DIVINE items are originally MYTHIC and need a [[Recombobulator 3000]] to become DIVINE.

# Reforging
Aliases: Armor Reforge, Armor reforge, Awkward, Blended, Brilliant, Candied, Clean, Deadly, Double-Bit, Equipment Reforge, Equipment reforge, Excellent, Fair, Fast, Fierce, Fine, Fortunate, Gentle, Godly, Grand, Great, Green Thumb (Reforge), Hasty, Heavy, Heroic, Keen, Light, Lucky, Lumberjack's, Lush, Menacing, Neat, Odd, Ominous, Peasant's, Pretty, Prospector's, Pure, Rapid, Reforg, Reforge, Reforged, Reforges, Rich, Robust, Rugged, Sharp, Shiny, Simple, Smart, Spicy, Strange, Sturdy, Superior, Titanic, Unpleasant, Unreal, Unyielding, Vivid, Wise, Zealous, Zooming
Categories: Mechanics, Reforging
The Blacksmith NPC
Reforging GUI
Reforging is a mechanic that allows the player to add additional stats to items such as [[Weapons]] and [[Armor]], similar to [[Enchanting]]. An item can have only one reforge at once, but they can be replaced. Reforges provide greater stat boosts (except those with negative stats) the higher the [[Rarity]] of the item is. Reforge stones can be used to apply reforges on an [[Anvil]]. Reforges cost [[Coins]] to apply, and some have [[Mining]] level requirements.
## Search
e [[Reforging/Search|v]]
Search script not loaded yet.
## Usage
Reforges are applicable to [[Swords]], [[Bows]], [[Armor]] pieces, [[Equipment]] pieces, [[Pickaxes]], [[Farming Tools]], [[Axes]], [[Fishing Rods]], and [[Fishing Nets]], with a unique pool of reforges available for each item type. They can be applied through either a basic reforge or a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]]. They grant stats, which scale based on the rarity of the item.
The stats from reforges apply only while holding or wearing the item.
When an item with a reforge is upgraded, its reforge is usually kept. However, there are some exceptions, such as a [[Vampire Mask]] and a [[Witch Mask]] merging to a [[Vampire Witch Mask]]. Players can hover on the output slot of the crafting table to see if the reforge is going to be kept or not.
### Basic Reforging
Items can be reforged in the reforging menu, accessed from the [[Blacksmith]] or [[The Hex]].
The reforge given is random, with all reforges applicable to the item having the same chance to be rolled. Players will know the reforge given only after the reforge has been paid for.
The player's first Reforge is available for 10 [[Coal]], but all subsequent Reforges will cost the player 500 [[Coins]].
### Reforge Stones
[[Reforge Stones]] are items which grant special reforges to items. They can be applied through the [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] and cost [[Coins]] to apply, depending on the reforge stone rarity. Many also have a [[Mining]] level requirement to apply. They can be obtained through various means, such as mining, [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]], or as a drop from mobs.
[[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] applying cost depends on the stone's rarity:
|Stone Rarity|Price|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|5,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|

## Duplicate Prefixes
In addition to the reforges mentioned above, some [[Armor]] or [[Tools]] share a name with certain reforges. To avoid repeats ("Wise [[Wise Dragon Armor]]"), they use a different prefix (except "Shiny" [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]]). They still have the same stat bonuses.
Due to the changes in certain versions, some reforges are no longer available on the items (marked as "†").
|Reforge|Actual|Item|
|-|-|-|
|Very|Wise|[[Wise Dragon Armor]]|
|Very|Strong †|[[Strong Dragon Armor]]|
|Highly|Superior †|[[Superior Dragon Armor]]|
|Extremely|Heavy|[[Heavy Armor]]|
|Not So|Light|[[Heavy Armor]]|
|Thicc|Heavy|[[Super Heavy Armor]]|
|Absolutely|Perfect|[[Perfect Armor]]|
|Even More|Refined|[[Refined Mithril Pickaxe]]|
|Even More|Refined|[[Polished Titanium Pickaxe]]|
## Trivia
- This mechanic may be a reference to Terraria, as the game has similar reforging mechanics and names. A full list can be viewed here.
- The custom prefixes apply only to Armor pieces and Pickaxes, so it was possible to obtain a "Shiny [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]]".
- Before 0.8, the only reforge that had its stats in a different color instead of gray was Godly, with its stats being red. Now, all reforges are colored blue.
- The very first reforge on a profile costs 10 [[Coal]] instead of coins.
- Prior the Crimson Isle's update, accessories were reforgeable; the reforge system for accessory has been replaced with [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
